Cemetery Mystery

In-World18th of Talil, 1246 WhereVarious Locations in Emberstran PlayedJuly 16, 2025

Meeting with Lady Rook

Ky receives the hug, as Rook tells her she is not surprised to see her first - she expected it. They go to the top floor of the tower. Domed ceiling, floating lanterns. Can see the entire city from this vantage point. Polished table in the center of the room with moving symbols on it. Asks Ky about the forest. Ky gives her most of the info (but not all - no mention of the hidden chamber with the arcane waypoint).

She gives Ky a potion of invisibility - in thanks. Ky asks about her standing with the Etterset…which Rook says is good. Ky brings up Hap and his land petition. And also about his dad’s death and the mystery around it - was it the Cloak? She talks to her about politics - and she asks about Ky’s family (and drops that she knows her real name - which Ky quickly “corrects”) as the door opens and a silver-haired lady comes in. Imonarra enters and needs to speak to Lady Rook. Apologies are made as Rook invites Ky and companions to dinner she is hosting at the Old Oak & Grapes at twilight…with an interesting group of like-minded individuals. Ky leaves…in search of her friends who have not yet arrived.

At the Temple

Meanwhile, the others all meet up and talk at the Temple. Discuss that a coach and driver showed up. They notice as they talk that some people walk by and are whispering - clearly talking about the four of them. They talk about how good they all look.

Aury feels a tug on his cloak and it is the acolyte..who tells him that people in town are talking about them (forest cleared for hunters, helped with the vintners’ guild, were seen in the tower the day before). Aury tries to coach him and tells him that he is going to be sent twice a week to the captain of the guard (Vermond) for sword lessons. He is excited to hear the news - he’d love to be like Hap someday. Hap, pleased to hear this, creates a minor illusion to show him what he can look like. Ainslie, not to be outdone, gives the boy money (10gp) to go to the market to buy him a red outfit. Aury tells him that he needs to stay on the path of Diverra…as the lad says he will spread the good word about the EGD. He starts to walk away and then realizes he doesn’t have his blade so goes back to get one. When he comes back, Og says “Scarlett Knight - here you go and gives him a hand ax.”

They hear someone snickering nearby…big guy with leathers, grungy, with a bow and sword. “You must be the ones who lifted the blight.” Word travels fast because the hunters want to get back into the woods. He talked to Lady T…and told her that he saw the group heading into the forest shortly before the blight lifted…and presents a scroll from her in thanks: “Keep it up, Emberstran is proud.”

Ainslie opens the scroll. “To the champions…guild owes you gratitude. Requests presence at the Suncrest Cellarium this early afternoon (after lunch) to give thanks.” Aury knows that this is a special place in the hills near the vineyards…only people in the Guild use this place/get invited usually to taste fine wines, etc. He knows this is a big deal. About that time, Ky shows up.

Ky shares what happened at the Tower. They share what happened in the Temple. Discussion about whether or not to trust Rook. Og questions the arrival of the blight on the crops vs. the arrival of Rook. We find out that the blight arrived after Rook arrived. The Priestess told us that the blight was from Blackrend…and we don’t know who that is. We know we put out this instance of Blackrend’s influence.

More discussion. Ky tries to convey that we shouldn’t close any doors at this point with Lady T or Rook. Group goes to the Dyed Cane for breakfast…with Ky and Ainslie stopping by her room on the way for a little while (with the land records we gathered for Lady T about the vineyard in question). Ainslie brings up the need to have more attractive clothing to wear to the festivities today. We decide to go to the market before breakfast.

At the market, we look for a clothing shop/tailor. He recognizes us as the Heroes of Emberstran and offers us a 10% discount. Ky pays Ainslie 7gp; Ainslie pays the tailor 24gp for a white tux for Hap and a green form-fitting dress for Ky. Ky also buy a green cloak for 2sp. Aury buys a silk robe for 12gp - white priestly robes with Diverra red and roses. Group sees the acolyte in the market spending the money on the items he was supposed to buy.

Ky takes Og to Charum’s Oils. The rest of the group goes to Dyed Cane. Ky asks for Tessa - talk of our efforts. Tessa says we need “Bru” to fix up Og. Ky leaves Og there and goes to the Dyed Cane the others for breakfast. Bordi welcomes “the talk of the town.” Aury mentions we are there for breakfast and Bordi says he will set the group up. Bordi asks us about if we learned anything about the fragment…and we told him we are still working on it. Aury mentions the murder and Bordi says he heard that the lady was killed because she knew who was poisoning the wine - but could just be a rumor. He mentions her friend in the guild…but unsure if there is a connection there. Either way, the talk is that whoever poisoned the barrels is probably behind the murder.

Ainslie talks to everyone of their “metal” colors, piercing Haps’s horns, etc. Aury brings up to Bordi about our invitation to the Cellarium…and he is surprised we got invited…and would go dressed like we are. We tell him we have acquired new clothes. Aury more concerned with who might be there - guild members, nobles, perhaps the Lord himself…but Bordi doesn’t know. Ainslie asks for a private table…Bordi doesn’t grant it freely so we tell him nevermind. We discuss plans - Ainslie brings up perhaps bringing up the “dead body under the Lord’s Keep” to the Lord if we see him today. We decide to mention it as a “rumor” and to have Ainslie get his reaction. Ainslie would like to go back down there…but we decide to wait and see how the event goes tonight (are people otherwise engaged, do they get drunk, etc.). After breakfast, Hap goes back to the Temple. Aury goes to pick up his robes. Ainslie and Ky go to Ainslie’ room. Og wraps up at the Charum’s Oils and goes to pick up his armor.

As Ky and Ainslie walk in the Bear, people stand and clap…thanking them. They are hunters…and eager to get into the woods. When they get to the room, Ky gets the documents from Ainslie. She copies them - making a forgery of the main documents - to give to Lady T (what she asked for). The Cetaen family borrowed money from Benoit to get out of debt. Two days later, Benoit is dead. Ky forges the documents perfectly and Ainslie is able to “age” them as well. Ky then changes her clothing. Ainslie changes. Og comes back to the Bear - he feels magical and is glowing.

At the Temple, Hap changes into the tux. Aury goes back to the Temple and puts on his new robes. We all meet at the Temple and then head to the Cellarium.

Suncrest Cellarium

We head out into the vineyards…and the Cellarium is made of glass and golden stone up on the hillside. We are met with the aroma of wine, candle-lit chamber. Casks along the walls. A half-elf welcomes us and takes us to Lady T who welcomes us and encourages us to try the various wines. She expresses her gratitude and beckons us to sit and talk. Aury walks around to sample wines.

Lady T asks exactly how we rid the lands of the blight. Ainslie talks to her about how we did a lot of research, acquired an ancient artifact that helped us, Og’s ritual. Ainslie leaves out any mention of Lady Rook. Lady T then asks Aury about extra details…and specifically mentions that she thought we visited a grove of the goddess Diverra. Aury asks “exactly what do you want to know.” Lady T was so upset last time…and now things are looking up so she just wants to know what happened. Aury gives her a few additional details…that we were in the grove, that Og performed a ceremony, perhaps to Diverra…and that with Diverra’s guidance we were able to rid the blight. She introduces the half-elf as a sommelier named Vargaro. She then brings up the issue of the Cetaen family and Ky tells her we got her the records she needed. Ky gives them to Hap, who gives them to her. She then reads and says that “Benoit” might be the rightful heir to the lands. Then she sees that Benoit died. We then tell her that Benoit had heirs. Hap tells her that he had a daughter named Phira (Ky mentions he had a boy, too).

Lady T, after some thought, seems to recall that Phira passed away a few years ago (maybe in 1241…5 years ago)...and is buried in Crael cemetery. Aury thinks that Lady T is unsure of her story, but is being truthful. Hap asks what Lady T would do if we were to find the heir - esp. since Benoit and Phira might have been killed over the lands. She leans in close and says she’s not sure because she’s building a case for how she can keep these lands out of the hands of the person who is trying to buy it. Aury, drunk, says to Hap…so if your “sister is buried in the cemetery…” and Ainslie tries to cover with the use of the word “sister” by a priest.

She says there are votes that don’t support me out there. My stewardship may not last long in the guild, but she asks us for our support to help her keep her position. Ainslie brings up Lady Rook…and she claims she hasn’t met her…but that Rook is the one who wants to buy the lands. Hap brings up “are these records” what you wanted us to bring you. Ky brings up the fact that the lands could be owned by the heir…and that wouldn’t necessarily influence who is the steward of the guild. Lots of conversation about Rook (Lady T thinks she is nefarious), the lands, whether or not the Cetaen family is good or bad, etc. The patriarch of the Cetaen family - she heard - may have had Benoit killed. Ky asks if Verellen is a member of the Cloak. She says that the Cloak has been here a long time - before Emberstran became what it is. She may have even had dealings with the Cloak herself, because she had to. She offers to pay us double for the records - she gives us each 25gp for providing the records. We tell her that we will do some additional research about the heir. Ky asks her what she would do if she was displaced. She doesn’t know. We thank her for the hospitality and leave.

We walked back to the Temple. Talk of keeping Hap’s identity a secret for a little longer. We talk of going to the cemetery to see if we can find Phira’s grave…and even speak of “speaking with the dead.” We discuss who we are going to support - Lady T or Lady R or neither. Ky knows that the Cloak are the “hammer” in this town - not just the hired assassins. We decided to go to the cemetery to find Phira. Ainslie would like to stealth around the Lord’s Keep to look for any connections to evil or any major influences (Cloak, Etterset, etc.).

The Cemetery

We change and then go to Crael’s Cemetery. On the way, Darn (dockhand) runs into us. Is it true - you guys are responsible for so many good things in this town? Ainslie says yes. Darn says that this info was handed out by the Town Crier this morning (Ainslie confirms that the Crier is given news from the Lord). Vamod comes out and talks to Og - “great job - I knew you had it in you to be this helpful.” He asks Og (and the group) to join his team to keep the streets of Emberstran safe. Og declines - says he is “rocking with this crew” now. We continue on.

We make our way into the cemetery and the guards ask us why we are there. Ainslie says on official EGD business and asks if they will keep folks out…and they said they will try. Ky asks about who might know where folks are buried and they tell us that Guy, the groundskeeper, will know. We decide to split up to look for gravestones. Ky finds stones for Phira and Benoit…and next to them Eldermer Cetaen. Ainslie finds Guy…his name is actually Rono. Ainslie compliments him on his work. Rono starts to tell him all about his craft. Ainslie asks him about burial procedures. Rono says he is the one who digs the holes, maintains the grass and grounds (no weeds). Rono blames the cultists for the discoloration of the grass (Eradic of Ghone). Ainslie then leads him over the Phira’s and Benoit’s graves. Rono says the body in Benoit’s grave does not have horns. Ainslie pays him 1gp. He finds out that Eldermer’s body is buried in Benoit’s grave (he says he was paid to put that body in that grave). Ainslie pays him an additional 1gp. Rono says that the Phira grave is actually empty…no one is buried there. Ainslie paid him another gp. Rono says that he is paid to do various things and always has been. Ruthless Jerik is the one who paid him back then to bury folks where and how he did.

Jae is Dead

Ainslie then walks over to the group and tells us what happened. Ky asks Aury if he knows who Ruthless Jerik is. He does not, but knows that people have often referred to a “” as someone of influence in the town. On the way to dinner, we swing by to see Jae. He is not there. Ainslie opens the door (which is unlocked). We see bloody footprints and Jae’s body on the ground. We see Jae has been stabbed in the back. Ky determines the footprints look like someone who is 3’ tall…and stride seems to match the prints. Ainslie starts to investigate the scene and finds that some books have been disturbed. He casts detect magic and senses “transformation” magic hidden underneath the floor near where Jae previously made tea (when he was still alive the first time we visited). He finds a trap door and finds a small wooden box with 15pp inside, as well as a small vial - crimson liquid that pulses like a heartbeat. Og left to find Vamod….and comes back without him. A few moments later, another captain comes in - what are you doing here? We tell him that we went to report the murder of Jae (this is the captain of the SW). We ask him to stay on scene until Vamond arrives. We ask him to leave us out of this until we solve the murder. Aury asks if he will make sure the body is delivered to the Temple of Diverra. Ainslie says - or you can take him to Guy.

Dinner with the Lady

We make our way to the Old Oak & Grapes. As we walk by the Temple, we see the acolyte outside. Og encourages him to do push ups and we keep going. As we arrive, we are met by an attendant who shows us in and guides us around through a narrow archway behind which is a tapestry of a weeping dryad. There is a room here with a chandelier and a table with goblets that are filled with wine. Lady Bodiva Rook is here (black silk dress). “You’ve come.” Lots to discuss and great wines, too. Folks here are: Ehkel an’Davo (dark hair and skin, polished leathers). Before we start, do you have the stone? Og says it is here and takes it out…but says he has questions first. Ky asks who Master an’Davo is - he is a traveller. And she asks if she can make sure that the blight is in the stone. She looks at it. “You’ve done something remarkable here…it may be more potent than I imagined.” She is interested in this stone. Aury thinks that no one is getting out of here without her having that stone. She reaches to touch the marrowstone and it flashes an indigo, brighter than we’ve ever seen before. Ainslie surroundings suddenly disappear…he sees 2 people: a golden mantle, familiar to him with spells arched around him in flame, the other dark robed adversary (flail with magical stars) as he hears “bound, bound and not broken”. Lady Rook didn’t flinch at the flash. “It is what I expected - shall we eat.” Ainslie feels like the vision connected him with the man.

– The End of the Session –

Sides & 3rd Piece

In-World18th of Talil, 1246 WhereThe Old Oak & Grapes in Emberstran PlayedAugust 11, 2025

Dinner with Lady Rook

Ky asks - what was that flash? Lady Rook-perhaps something to do with the blight - to that end, perhaps we should let Imonarra study it? Og says yes - that would be good, we aren’t smart enough to figure that out, but perhaps we should give it to her ourselves. Lady Rook -I detect a little mistrust, but that would be okay with me.

And with that dinner is served - rich foods, very nice, nice red wine. Ehkel decides to entertain us with a story. Trailport - breed fast, spirited horses. Ghost Gray - was a special horse who would watch the road like she was waiting for something. Then pauses - Hap - you look familiar - but maybe not. And he continues. The horse followed him for 10 miles…but in the morning, she was gone. He proposes a toast - to things that follow trust and others that follow silence. Ky then makes a toast to odd friends - and talks a little about how she and Og became friends such that she followed him to Emberstran. He asks for additional stories and no one speaks up. So then he asks us where we plan to go after dinner and we talk about where everyone is staying. He is at the Anchor Point (docks). Ainslie then asks how old he was during the Awakening (Ehkel is now in his 40s). He says around that time he was in the Mahruud. Og asks what brings him to Emberstran. He says it was Lady Rook. They met a decade ago - he says Rook is reliable. Hap brings up how Rook brought up that she knows something about Hap’s family - did she mention that to you - is this a good time to talk about this?

Aury interrupts to tell a story about a merchant named Phalen in another town. He tells that Phalen did not trust spell weavers. And notes how several magic wielders have taken interest in town…politics, costs of things around town, other conflicts…and the mages had been the mayor’s advisors. Phalen went to the mayor and mentioned how magic should serve the town…vs. serving but a few. Ehkel comments that Rook has chosen friends well.

Rook then asks Og where he got his thorn leaf. He tells how he does odd jobs and on one job was drawn to a cave…where he then found this special leaf. Ky comments how it was fated.

Ehkel then talks about he was chasing a stag and found a green-eyed woman, Dandelia of the Veil in Kahlbit’s Veil (name always changed). She told him that the forest keeps it own keys. And Og’s story made him think of that. Maybe she would be able to help you figure out the origin of your leaf.

Ky asks him if he is member of the Advents of Harmony? He says no - he is more spontaneous. And then says that makes him think of Hap again. And how familiar he looks. Is he sure he has never been to Trailpoint. He then talks about a lady who looks very much like Hap who he met in Trailpoint. This woman said she impulsively left the city to buy the inn in town. Ky says that it was the Shimmering Stag Inn…she’s been there before. But she doesn’t remember the proprietors of the establishment. Ky comments that she is sorry she can’t place them since this could be her sister.

Hap then asks Rook what she knows about his family. She comments that the documents indicate that he and his sister are the rightful owners of the land.

She then says if we choose to support her, that property that she purchased could find its way back into Hap’s hands. Ky mentions that we have documentation that would show that the land already belongs to Hap. She thinks that would be great - and thinks that Hap should try to find his sister. Ky then shows Rook the documentation (the copy that she made)...and Rook says that is wonderful. She says that we should meet with the magistrate to formalize that the land belongs to Hap. Again - asking for our support.

Hap brings up that we met with Lady T. And Rook talks about how she is of the old ways, but that she helped us stop the blight. Hap says that he just hates to see bad things happen to either one. Ky then asks why she doesn’t set her sights on higher leadership. And she doesn’t want all of the responsibility. She wants to help the Lord with trade, raising an army…making things better.

Aury is skeptical of everything. Hap tries to be neutral. Ky asks what will happen to Lady T if we support Rook. For example, would she make sure that Lady T still has a position of some importance? Rook says she hasn’t thought about it, but would hope that Lady T wouldn’t be vengeful.

Hap asks where the wine is from. This establishment always gets the oldest and best. Hap wants to know which vineyard. Ky asks where an’Davo is heading - he had already said across the Glimmering Sea but didn’t say where and asks us for recommendations. We have none. Og asks if he ever found the horse - and he never did.

Ky escorts them out. Asks Rook if she can help with a favor…and asks her if she can ask Immonarra to send a message to the proprietor in Trailpoint…trying to find out who she is (a family member wants to find her). Rook says that she will help and hopes the group will take her favor to heart. She then tells an’Davo that he should visit the dwarven areas to the SE of Mount Emberstran. He catches her meaning and asks if he should take a guide…and she says to look her up and perhaps she can take him.

Ainslie disappears after they leave and follows an’Davo to Charum’s Oils…and then comes out to talk to him. He asks if he saw the flash when Og showed the stone to Rook. He said he just saw the flash and nothing else, but Ainslie reveals that he saw a vision. - and he told Ainslie not to tell anyone else the vision, and that this is something that has been played out before. And he asks an’Davo’s advice on what to do. He suggests that he finds an archivist that he can trust to get more info about that vision. He also told Ainslie that there is probably someone in the town who already knows who Ainslie is…even though he hasn’t told anyone. But he doesn’t say that is Lady Rook. He thinks it is probably someone else.

They make it to Charum’s Oils. An’Davo walks up to one individual and asks for a private session from a petite female human (Tessa) and offers to pay for a session for Ainslie - asking that he gets the Pearl Oil and a female tiefling (Kia) shows up to help him. She applies Pearl Oil (looks like inside of oyster) to Ainslie as she tends to him….his skin has a glow of that when finished. “You must have a friend - this was a gp for this special oil.” She asks Ainslie who An’Davo is - is he new in town? She seems to know Tessa. Ainslie says he is in with the nobles…and she says that makes sense. Ainslie asks her not to reveal what she has seen under his clothes and tips her well. She says she will keep the secret.

Hap writes a letter and gives instructions to Fleur:

Instructions: Head to Trailpoint and find the Shimmering Stag Inn. Spend a day looking for a Tiefling woman that resembles ole Hap. If found, give her the letter and wait for a verbal response. Otherwise, return.

Hap and Aurelius discuss going back to the portal to see what is on the other side.

Aury writes a letter to the lord …

Ky asks Og and Hap what they thought about the information about Hap’s sister.

Hap says he has never met anyone who would buy land and then give it away. Ky tells him that there are people like that - those who are so wealthy that they just look for an entry into a town. Discussion about the land and Hap would be happy but okay if it doesn’t work out. He is going to write a letter and send it with his owl to Trailpoint. Plans to send it tomorrow. Ky mentions that she didn’t want to show the original document to Rook (or anyone yet) and Aury catches this and asks about it…which Ky says it would be best if he didn’t know. Og talks to Hap about Phira and what it might mean if he finds her.

Meanwhile…Ky goes back in and Aury hands her a sealed letter addressed to the Lord, asking her to take it to her contacts to make sure it gets to the Lord.

Discussion about what to do next - visit the portal of Diverra and investigate further (Hap and Aury). Ky mentions that we need to decide about the Rook vs Lady T issue. Ainslie comes back in with a tale about why he was gone for a long time - leading to him going to the bath house. He says that he has decided to support Rook - better to back the stronger horse, than the one limping to the finish line. Og said that even if he felt a little used by Rook, she scratched his back so he is fine to back her. Hap says he doesn’t understand why anyone would value his opinion. Aury doesn’t see anything wrong with Lady T, but thinks Rook is seeking ambition above her station. He wants to support neither.

Back to what to do. Take stone to Imonorra…ask Rook about solving the murders…give Aury’s letter to Lord. Ky goes to Charum’s Oils. Aury and Hap go to the Temple. Og and Ainslie go to Jae’s house to investigate.

Ky - sees an’Davo come out with Tessa and he recommends her to Ky…then pays for Ky. She asks Tessa about Rook. Tessa said the an’Davo thinks that Rook was supposed to be the hit at the show that night. And that the hit was the Cloak of Defiance. She says that “the whisper” did it (via poison in a drink). Whisper is a small female who disguises herself. Whisper likely killed Jae as well. As a member of the Cloak, they don’t want any changes in the town. So they would want Lady T to stay in her position. Tessa liked an”Davo - great stories (lost caravan, etc.). Ky leaves - taking nothing - heads to bed.

Temple

Aury looks for the Priestess and finds her lighting candles. She is so happy to have heard the stories and says that she has something to give the “champions of the Ardent One.” She gets a key and leads Aury down to a place in the temple he’s never been…large oak door (Hap went to bed). This is a vault with magical sconces, lots of scrolls, banners, many rose items. She goes to a low chest (large) with the sigil of Diverra on it. She opens it and inside is a breast plate that needs to be inscribed with Diverra’s new symbols. It is polished and silver and warm to the touch. This will help with any additional efforts for your assistance with Emberstran. Aury tries to get out of taking it, but she insists. Aury noticed a broken piece of tablet in a bag on a shelf. She sees Aury looking at it. “That old thing - it has been here for years, and it seems important so we kept it.” She says that Aury can take it with him if he takes the breast plate. And I hope you have more strength at the Silver Spaulder than I had….I hope your prejudices do not pass on from me. She hands Aury the third piece of the tablet wrapped in cloth. Aury heads to bed.

Jae’s house

Og and Ainslie show up - EGD - here on investigative business. What do you know - where are the reports. Vamod has them - his team investigated. Why do you need to be here now? We had other business to attend to. They try to talk the guy into leaving. They follow the small footprints down the road and off the edge of the dock. They ask about another record keeper…and are told the Magistrate (who is in the High District). They discuss how the murderer probably fled to the sewers to escape. They then go back to the Bear. Innkeeper - the hunters are back in and I’ve saved your key…they are coming back because of you guys. Some discussion about the price of their room, saving their room, etc. They go to bed.

19th of Talil

Everyone wakes up. Aury goes to see Hap with his new breastplate and also shares about the tablet (FLAME SECTION). Hap is excited to see the tablet. They want to share with the rest of the group. Aury looks for Arnol the Acolyte…but he isn’t there.

Ky gets up early and walks to the Tower. Datheril greets and gets Rook. She asks them to convey Aury’s message to the Lord. Imonorra will take it today. She then tells her that she and some of her companions will support her. And finally she talks about the Whisper and solving the murders. Rook conveys that it will take some time for Rook to out the Cloak for those crimes. Rook asks that we stand down the investigation of the murders. But also says that if Hap is upset and seeks vengeance against the Cloak…let her know how she can help. She then offers to take Ky to Imonorra. They go to the top of the tower. Pardon me sister - and Rook then asks for her help to get Ky to Trailpoint to validate. Imonorra takes her via a teleport spell to the Spinning Stag Inn. Ky goes inside the Inn and finds the owners. There is a human male and a tiefling female. Ky approaches the female and it is indeed Phira…Ky tells her everything. She is very scared for Hap…says that he needs to leave. Ky tells her that she is aware of the Cloak threat. And that Jerik is her husband’s brother. And gives Ky a ring and an apron to take to Hap….says to return with the ring someday.

Bear - the Town Crier is there announcing that the blight is gone. Trade is strong. Coin is high. Vendors are happy. Stop by the Temple…lady luck. Sailors mock him. A man (ordinary in dress, but uneasy) slips up in the crowd and stands next to Ainslie (you feel it don’t you…he says…the weight of being seen even when you wish you weren’t. Carrying things that don’t belong to you. We don’t want to hurt you. If you tire of being a vessel of their lies come find the silence beneath the stones…the ones who understand). He carries a bone carved token dangling from a thin cord - cult representing Sijulous Ghone. Ainslie knows that Eridict is a group of cultists of Sij Ghone…and feels like he was just recruited by them to join. Ainslie knows that SG is an all powerful immortal wizard. Ainslie tells Og what is going on in his head. Og watches and sees the guy go into an alley…and looks back to see if Ainslie is following him. He knows that the two are tracking him. They go down alleys to the east of town…and come up to an area in the shadow of the cemetery. A boarded up building with cracked shutters and an iron gate is there…they need to go around back…if interested in joining this cult. They then share that they learned that the tracks at Jae’s house lead into the water. Ky then shares all she learned about the murders and Cloak.

Discussion about everything. We all agree that the Cloak is bad. Agree that the Whisper needs to be taken care of privately; that we cannot take them out publicly. Blight has a weird association with the thorn leaf (which we can talk to the druid about).

The Sewers

In-World19th of Talil, 1246 WhereThe Dyed Cane in Emberstran PlayedAugust 20, 2025

Inside the Dyed Cane

Sitting at Bordi’s (Dyed Cane). Lella is our server. We eat, decide to head to the sewers - to find the Whisper/Cloak and question about the murders (of a girl at Old Oak and Grapes, Jae) and to look for the fourth tablet. We decide to go to the Temple of Diverra to look for a sewer entrance to avoid having eyes on us entering the sewers since it is morning. Aury isn’t sure where the sewer entrance might be…but also didn’t know about the vault.

Ky hears the shuffle of coins in the box and she looks for the key. She comes over and offers to unlock the door, asking Aury to stay here by the door in case we come back.

Into the Sewers

The door has not been opened in a long time (makes a sucking sound). There is a ladder heading down. We notice pipes from sewage that dump into the sewers (the smell is also horrid). Aury will stay and the password will be “The Flower Blooms”...for him to reopen it with the key.

Ky starts down and starts to gag, but is not impacted by the odor (poisonous). Ainslie makes nose clamps for the other three. Ky sees slope as she gets to the bottom of the ladder. We will have to trek through muck. Hap goes next - also okay. Ky gathers her bearings - knowing she needs to go north. Ainslie and Og then come down - they are okay, too. Ky and Hap stay about 40’ ahead of the other two.

Rats

As they head north and reach the T, they see/hear rats. IT IS INITIATIVE.

Hap goes first and crits, taking one out. Then Ky kills one. One attacks Hap, and hits. Then another attacks him and hits. Og dashes and things happen. Ainslie goes, things happen, a rat dies. Hap goes, misses, hits though does not kill, hits again and kills. Combat ends.

Ky looks into the alcove where they came from - there is a skeletal creature (chewed up) in there. It has been a few days at least. Ky opens the pouch - it has coins (3gp, 12sp - splits on the spot). Ainslie cleans it up for Ky. Hap looks in the backpack…there is a tender box, 4 torches, 2 flasks of oil (split all on the spot). Ky decides to crawl into the rat tunnel to check out the remaining scattered gear, looking for clues who this person was. She finds a lock picking kit (which Hap takes), bedroll in pieces, another piece of cloth…then a nest of bones and two babies (which she kills with remorse). On the cloth - which is a cloak - she sees the symbol of the Cloak of Defiance. She backs out and shares what she found.

Lockjaw & Dust

The group then proceeds to the left to a dead end, finding nothing. The group goes back to the T and heads east. Nothing much to find other than holes that drain into the sewers…and an occasional grate that allows daylight to filter in. Come to a T - to the left is an immediate dead end (large storm drain) and dead end to the right…so we follow the path east again, until we encounter a circle. Flow of sewage is slow…there has been no rain lately. Ky and Hap notice a flicker of firelight to the right side of the circle. Ky motions to Hap to be still…that she will try to stealth that way (and telepathically tells Ainslie/Og to still). Ky sees a guy in a hood/cloak with a lantern. This is Lockjaw. He sees Ky (draws a bow on her) and the two converse. Ky tells them that she is with companions looking for their “priest” friend. He says he is looking for a friend, too. Ky tells him she found someone and that she will take him to him. His companion Dust shows up…and Ky takes them to the dead body (encountering her companions along the way). They confirm the dead body was their acquaintance (Smiley).

Ky introduces the group…but uses Hapenstance McGrady (McG) for Hap’s name. They tell us to turn around because Jerik won’t be happy we are in the sewers. Then they mention Quill - is a “she.” The Black Quill…to us. Ainslie asks about the Whisper. We know that they know of Quill. They say they are going to tell Quill we are down there. Hap tells them to hold on…that we need to see Jerik. They say that Jerik is too busy and won’t give us the time of day. They insinuate that Quill is under Jerik. As we are trying to talk them into not leaving, Ainslie casts a spell, which hits Lockjaw psychically. It is INITIATIVE.

Lockjaw attacks Ky and hits once. Og then moves up and shoves him, and does great damage to him. Ainslie leans forward to cast a spell at Dust and hits, killing him. Lockjaw interacts to beg for his life and Hap says if he will answer questions he can live. The guy asks to put his weapons away and pulls out a vial and holds it to his mouth. We ask who/where Quill, Jerik and Whisper are. He will not tell us. We ask why the girl was killed at the Old Oak and Grapes…it was because she knew of the plot to kill Lady Rook. Ky asks for confirmation: Jerik ordered the death of Rook, but that hasn’t happened yet. Imonorra (he spits), Magistrate and Hand…and others are ok with the Cloak. What does Quill look like? She is half-elf that blends in with the nobility…she changes her appearance frequently. He says he will leave the town if we will let him. Ky offers to fake his death. He says we might be able to get some bodies from the cultist group. He says they are sick - hanging out in the cemetery. If the Eradic of Ghone gets your body…they will give your soul to Sijulous. We ask him to strip off his weapons and cloak. He can keep his money…he says he will board a ship and leave. Ainslie asks if we will encounter other Cloak members in the sewers. Ky asks for his real name…Dren Velt is his real name.

We then take the loot from Dust (split it) and drag his body to the rat alcove. Ky retrieves the rat eaten cloak and then Ainslie sets fire to the alcove. The plan is to be able to tell people that we found an infestation of rats that looks to have killed 3 Cloak of Defiance members (cover for Lockjaw). Hap (Ivory) puts on Dust’s Cloak, Og - Lockjaw’s cloak (Biggie) and Ainslie puts on Smiley’s tattered Cloak (Talon) don cloaks. Ainslie takes Ky’s clothes and rips them up to match Smiley’s cloak. Ky pulls up her cloak (Smalls). Should we encounter anyone then we are Cloak of Defiance from Paendley…Smiley asked us to come (he was a childhood friend with Edge (rank) Rock our superior.

We go back to the circle and head clockwise around the circle (toward a). We find a passage on the left (northwest) and follow it to the dead end at the guard house at the Upper City Gate. We see a ladder that leads up into a trap door into the guard house. We go back to the circle and head clockwise. We take the next passage to the left (northeast). Ainslie leads, followed by Og, Ky and then Hap in the back.

Down the passage, we notice there is no sludge…this is a clean passage. We start to travel to the next circle. Ainslie fails a DEX Saving Throw…his body is pulled forward and starts to float in the air, with acid on his skin– restrained.

Gelatinous Cube

IT IS INITIATIVE: Combat vs. a Gelatinous Cube
  • ROUND ONE: Og has to make a dex check and succeeds. Og then uses his glaive to swirl around the floating Ainslie…looking to see what is holding Ainslie up. This action lodges his weapon and he calls that out. Ky then looks and doesn’t see anything…and jumps up on top of Ainslie and finds herself stuck and with acid damage. Hap then attacks the air with a firebolt and does damage…and reveals that they are stuck in a gelatinous cube. Ainslie takes damage. Ainslie then uses his telepathic feat to shove the cube away from him (Ky and Og’s weapon are still inside)…dislodging him from it. He then casts a spell and does damage.
  • ROUND TWO: The cube tries to hit Ainslie and misses. Og attacks with another weapon and hits. Then tries to punch it and do additional damage…getting his hand stuck inside. Ky attacks from inside with disadvantage and hits, then attacks with an offhand dagger and kills it.

Ainslie cleans us up. We see rusty weapons, 1pp (we split), a piece of topaz (50gp to split later when sold - Ky keeps). We decide to take a short rest to bandage ourselves. WE THEN GO BACK AND GET AURY…AS WE KNOW IT IS DANGEROUS HERE AND WE NEED HIM. As we do, we hear critters, but don’t encounter anything. After a while, the muck comes back. We go around the next circle…changing the marching order: Ky, Og, Ainslie, then Hap. This area is a little wider and cleaner-probably cleaned by the cube. We then come to a passage on the left - due east. It is a little darker. Ky notices some cracks in the walls (normal size rats, spiders). Ky and Hap look for hidden doors/passages in this area…find nothing unusual.

The Scraping

We go that way and hear scraping metal on rock. Ky stealths down and sees someone trying to get into I’s Tower, messages to the group to catch up. Ky gets to the bottom of the ladder and looks up and sees a 3’ tall figure wearing a Cloak of Defiance cloak and plague doctor mask…they drop the pick axe.

Confronting Cloak

In-World20th of Talil, 1246 WhereThe Sewers of Emberstran PlayedSeptember 9, 2025

Aury’s “cloak” nickname is Flower. He is with us.

  • ROUND ONE: Ky asks her to stand down and then fires her crossbow and hits. The figure throws two marbles that shift into rats. Aury and Hap study the crystal and ear cuff (Listener’s Coil). Ainslie studies the mask and takes it (Mask of Withered Breath). Ky takes the Coil as well (party inventory).

Near the Crimson Swan. The Emberstan mercenaries have been sent to investigate. We ask her if we should help and she says yes.

Hap sends a note via Fleur to Phira (asking about Rook).

They tell her they work for Captain Vamond and that they want to help. She rushes over to the man and says Jerik - stop for a minute. They can’t hear what she says, but they hear his response is “nothing.” She comes back over and says she won’t need their services. Ainslie mentions the lightning and she says there is nothing to see here. The case is solved. They say they are freelance workers. He will take them in - I think it will be perfect” Torrick: “what does she want?” Torrick has a jacket full of pockets (ink stains). “I guess I’ll talk to them” Torrick then comes over to talk to them.

They find out that he works for Jerik.

They are trying to figure out if they are being watched or followed. They start to walk away and someone comes up to them. What are you doing? I thought you were the heroes. Why would you get messed up with those people? And the guy walks away. Ainslie and Og walk to the Tower to wait for us.

And that maybe they are now persons of interest. Aury talks to them about the EGD and how people may question that they are not part of a real group.

She says she will talk to others in the Temple to ask around and that hopefully the two of them will get to the bottom of this.

Aury prays to Diverra. He gets a warm feeling as if her spirit is with him. Then a chill. As the prayer leads into what should I do…he gets warm again. He doesn’t get direct message. Just feels like he’s correct that something is wrong and that he needs to worry about him.

The Eradic

In-World21st of Talil, 1246 WhereVarious Locations of Emberstran PlayedOctober 7, 2025

At the Headless Hydra

Aury and Hap wake up and want to have breakfast…are told that the High Priestess wants to meet them. At the Warm Bear Inn

Lots of discussion…Ainslie trying to convince him to buy armor, Og saying he wants to wait. They discuss whether the money is a gift or a payment. They decide to wait for now to buy anything. As they walk, they decide to go to the Gems of Emberstran and talk to the proprietor (Befast Umberfist) about buying gems (500gp worth). Some discussion about trying to find pink gems. The guy is on the up and up. They agree to buy five 100gp rubies (much easier to carry than 500gp). They thank him and head to the temple.

Discussion about the EGD going vs. the entire group going. We decide to split up and some go shopping/selling while the EGD investigates. We decide to meet up at the Cane…if they find anything. Otherwise at the Temple in the morning.

Ky, Aury, and Hap head to the gem store. Nice store. We notice a dwarf going through a big box of coins. We see him shake off a look and then welcome us to the store. His assistant is short, gray gnome with violet eyes…quiet. Ky pulls out an opal. Emiffi - bring that to me. She says “you’ve got one, too” And Ky asks what she meant..but she doesn’t explain. He offers 10gp for the opal and Ky isn’t sure about the price. Aury thinks it is good. Emiffi is going crazy wanting to talk…and Aury drops to his knee and says “Child - are you ok.” She says - can you help her find the other side of her necklace? They notice that Ky is wearing a locket that is in half. He asks Ky if she is from around here and tells his name and Ky says that price is fair. Hap tries to intimidate Bafast…and doesn’t. Aury asks Emiffi if that is a fair price and she is squirmy…doesn’t contradict her boss. She is a deep gnome hanging out with a dwarf…Ky knows that Umberfist is a clan in Dunduaar. He agrees to get the gold and opens up the chest and gives Ky 11gp. Discussion about Ky’s locket. Bafast tells her that he had the other half of the locket…until it was taken. He walks over and locks the door - I can’t be talking about this. He says that a “cloak rat” came in and stole it. Emiffi saw who stole it. It was someone with a red cloak. Bafast says there is too much that is strange around here…I’m kind of trapped. Caught between two stones. Ky tells him they are not affiliated with the Cloak. He says that he gets shipments…he wants to see the town where it should be. He isn’t happy about having to be here. Lady Rook has pressured him into supplying materials to the drow at the Spaulder (iron, steel, and dunduaar). He is not comfortable sharing more right now. He doesn’t know what Rook’s plans are for the drow. Ky asks him to acquire the locket if he can…and hold it for her. Ky says that she doesn’t like the Cloak either, but is on the fence about Rook. He’s not convinced that Ky is not going to turn on him. They leave and head to the Silver Spaulder.

The go in. Well kept. Suits of armor. We see two guys who look just like Emiffi…they are muscular; and a tall silvery-white haired, dark skinned individual. Ky tells him they have armor to sell. Aury says he has a chain shirt to sell. How much to trade chain shirt, Hap’s chain set for the splint mail? He makes an offer that is not fair, calling the trade chain “shitty.” Hap tries to persuade him…and does. 120gp and the items in exchange for the splint mail. Wimplerags…one of his helpers gets the items. Aury offers him a mace in exchange. Hap wears a white tux…while he waits for the armor to be finished up later today.

They walk out and head to the Rarities shop. Shop area and warehouse area. Half-orc guy with glaive guarding the place. Half-elf walks up to help us “looking for something you didn’t know you need?” Aury likes the sound of it. Ky says that she dropped something and will be right back. Aury and Hap believe her. Ky leaves. Aury says he didn’t know what he wanted. Raddic mentions that he needs a censer. This one has holy water and will shower someone (for one round it has self levitation) is 30gp. Aury buys it. She asks if Hap needs anything. She asks if people ever mistake him. He says no. She then says I hope I don’t offend, but I have this balm of removal…you rub this on your horns and they are not there for an hour. The balm is 5gp.

Ky goes back into the silver shop. Tries to ascertain what the deal is with this guy and Bafast and how that ties to Rook. He says that it is a business deal, but Ky isn’t able to get to the whole truth. She knows that he isn’t paying Bafast much for the supplies he’s getting from Dunduar. And that he doesn’t share the profits with him. And that he and Rook go “way back,” both arriving in town around the same time. She learns that his assistants and Bafast’s assistant are brothers/sister. Ky storms out and goes to the rarities shop and convinces Raddic to sell Hap the balm for 3gp and she buys it for him. The group then leaves and heads to the Dyed Cane.

Eradic of Ghone Hideout

Og and Ainslie follow the group and see them going into the gem store. Then proceed to the Eradic of Ghone area. They go to the door and see that it is locked. Og knocks…many times. Someone opens the door “we just paid our taxes last week” and then shuts the door. They knock again. Someone opens it. We are here for recruitment. “For he who is great…he who cannot be killed.” The guys asks who recruited them…and they describe Arnol. The guy says you can come in but need to stay in the front area (5’x10’ area). Area is unkempt, cobwebs, etc. The guy leaves them in there and goes through another door and locks it. Og hears some noises coming from another room (very odd). After a bit, the door opens and they are asked “are you committed.” They say yes and they are admitted through the door. There is a hall with many doors and a staircase. They are told to go into another room, take off all your belongings (not clothing), and stand in the middle of the room and wait for us. Og challenges “naked” and they say no - just take off your backpacks, pouches, etc., and maybe sit or kneel. As the door closes, they see Arnol walk past. Ainslie says in his head “how’s it going” and he responds “it is going fine.”

They take off their belongings and do as asked. A little while later people come in dressed in purple cloaks. “You have chosen to bring forth Sijulous” - and Ainslie responds “praise be” but they say that is over the top. They say they are going to go to the cemetery to dig up bodies to try to capture the trapped souls for their god. You will need to wear these robes. Your belongings stay here. Ainslie thinks that these people will go through their stuff so he reaches out to Arnol to come and watch it all. Arnol says - do you report to me now? And he says yes. Arnol says you know they are going to take it to the caves. That stuff is no longer yours. Ainslie and Og have a conversation in their heads about not giving stuff away. They offer to let him keep the glaive. They start to resist. Ainslie tells them they need to fight. “Rip and Tear.” Og goes over to get his glaive and then surprises the guy. IT IS INITIATIVE.

ROUND ONE: Og kills the guys immediately. Ainslie says “Your soul for Sijiulous.” The guy makes a noise that travels out of the door. They close the door, put their belongings on, then put the cloaks on over them.

They take the dagger and put it into his chest and smear the blood on the ground in a circle. There are two guys outside the doors who ask what the noise was…and Ainslie says one of their fellow members wanted to dedicate his life to the cause. Ainslie is able to deceive him. The guy comments that “he jacked up the recruiting room.” Says he will go and notify “Vada.” Ainslie says they will take the stuff downstairs, um I mean across the hall. Og and Ainslie see them go through another door (not up the stairs)...the door on the left. They go into the room across the hall and see open crates with stuff thrown in all (clothes, daggers…nothing of value that they can see). They throw the dead cultist’s stuff in the crates. They then leave the room and go down the hall to the door on the far right. It is empty, save bed rolls. They listen at the door where the guys went. Og hears a door close. They open the door and see an empty room, except for a few crates with food and wine. They don’t see another door. So they look for another door - since they heard one close. After a few minutes they find a trap door in the floor…with a ladder leading down to the sewers.

Once down, they think about the map and think they are dropping in at point K. Once down, they see the long sewer hall ahead. Og notices a small symbol sitting on one of the stones with a small skull with a crown on it. It is dark down here and Ainslie can’t see anything. Og describes what he sees, but neither is sure what it means. Og goes over and touches the symbol. When he pushes, it sinks into the stone and the outline of the bricks open and leads to a path, under the city….maybe directly under the cemetery. Ainslie and Og proceed forward with Ainslie holding on to Og’s shoulder.

As Aury, Hap, and Ky walk - they talk about her necklace, she admits it is tied to her family (and Hap says, like my ring). She says she is confused why the Cloak of Defiance would want the other half. And also tells them that she went to get more info from the silversmith about the deal with Bafast. She commits to Aury that she will try to gather more info to try to get to the bottom of Rook’s involvement. They then get to the Cane and Bordi gives them the regular table, asking Ky “are we all good.” Same for Aury. They sit and eat.

Back at the Eradic of Ghone, Ainslie asks Arnol via message what the passwords are for the cult group. Arnol is confused and doesn’t answer directly. Says he misses Aury. Says “I’m coming.” The guys light a torch and Arnol suddenly appears with a torch and Gertot leg (headed for someone else)…and they follow him. Arnol says “are they making you dig today” to the guys (not knowing who they are). And Og replies with a female voice.. Wow - you are a big woman, Arnol says.. Og tries to deceive him and doesn’t and then Arnol realizes who they are…and says welcome. Arnol asks if they are higher rank than him…and Ainslie tries to persuade him. Arnol says he has to drop off the leg with someone. They travel for some time, passing another ladder, as Og sees an outline of a door carved into the sewer wall (doesn’t seem to be carved by pick axes). Ainslie asks about the door and Arnol doesn’t know. They walk another 20’ and Arnol hands the meat to another cultist. The guy tips Arnol. He is not dressed in a robe, but a violet tabard (has sword,shield). He says “new recruits” and points down the hall. Arnol says “I thought you were higher rank” and the guy laughs as Og and Ainslie say they never said that. They decide to go into the door that they saw carved into the wall.

They open the door to a large, circular room with a runic circle. There is another robed figure in the room with a large creature (A chalyth – like an armadillo the size of a mastiff) with a skeleton in its maw and some acolytes here as well. The lady says “I am assuming you are here to proceed with the work, please proceed forward.” She points to another door. They go forward into the other room. The room is misshapen. There are two tunnels that lead from here. Lots of bone-skeletal creatures lying about. She says there are creatures floating, ghostly guys and two others with long tongues. She says she will hold them to allow them to pass and points to one of the tunnels. Ainslie says the person who recruited us killed himself and didn’t tell us what to do. She says no worries and gives them pick axes…Ainslie barely holds his so she says we messed up. And tells Og to take the ax into the tunnel…and he does. He follows the passage and sees others chipping away with axes. He sees debris of caskets and dead people. They are digging out the dead bodies from below. He gets the impression he is supposed to dig. He introduces himself saying he doesn’t know what he is doing. And they tell him to dig for bodies. He mentions a tablet and they have no idea…just say to dig for bodies. Meanwhile, Ainslie is with the lady. She says “so you can’t dig” and he says I can do magic and produces smoke in his hand. She says, can you detect magic and opens a door and tells him to “detect,” as he really thinks she wants him to truly detect magic. He casts the spell. And sees that this is funeral treasure…goblets, burial items, strings of pearls, other items. One thing stands out - the 4th piece of the tablet, as well as silver and gold items (urns, jewelry, a purse with coins). He thinks there are 500gp worth of valuable things. He also sees magic on the cultist lady (bracers-abjuration or enchantment). He starts to sort through items…picking things up and looking at them, appearing to study them. She watches him. He is trying to find a moment to grab the tablet as he studies and handles many items. Og tells him he has found three skeletons. He holds up the ivory symbol of Goldraen…and Ainslie says it seems to have divine power. He says he will continue to look. She says they will pray on that symbol…and use it for tonight’s ceremony. She says I’m assuming you will be at the ceremony tonight, right? If you find anything else, please let me know. Ainslie messages to Og to “get out,” as he pockets the tablet and then takes the onyx box (120gp) and starts to leave.

Og starts to head out of the tunnels, crouching as the tunnels are only 6’ tall. As he approaches the misshapen room…he casts gaseous form and floats through the room. As soon as Ainslie knows that Og is okay, he asks the lady if he can study the onyx box…saying he thinks there might be magic in it. She says, “good - I expect a response tomorrow of its capabilities.” The beast makes a noise and looks at Ainslie, but does nothing. Ainslie goes to the door to leave. They don’t see Arnol. Ainslie gets into another room and lights a torch. He sees the guy eating the leg and asks him how good it was, telling him that Arnol is good for helping him with anything he needs. The guy says “can he swim” and laughs and says I’ll ask him to swim - and points to a door. Ainslie asks him to open the door to show him. And he sees a 5’ wide tunnel that leads down the passage and he hears water. Thinks this might connect to the river that we know about. He says that she has a good guardian over there who will shoot anything that disturbs her. Ainslie and Og end up leaving the hideout - successfully.

Og feels remorse for grave digging…speaking about a child who was still decomposing. They walk away - a couple of hours have passed. It is not quite dinner time. They go to the Warm Bear Inn.

After a couple of hours, Hap and Aury go back to pick up the armor. The armor fits perfectly. He compliments Hap on his shape. Hap is happy. He asks where Ky is and Hap says she is finishing up eating. He asks him to pass a message to her that he will help “with the wooing of Bafast.”

Meanwhile, Ky goes to the gem store and asks Bafast what he knows of her necklace. Where did you get the half you had? He says it was a good luck charm that I’ve had for some time…given to me from my nephew (he’s not my nephew, but still). She asks if he was an Umberfist and he says no - a Cragfury. He says “they left us and I miss him and I want the other half of that back.” I don’t know where you got yours but I want mine back. She asks where they went and he says…the vein drove him away. She asked how he found himself involved in the enterprise. And he says that Rook persuaded him. She tells him he could get more coin. He says he hates the drow but likes the smith. She questions how the gnomes ended up working for both he and the drow. They had expertise in gems. When the vein was found and needed to be suppressed, the Cragfury disappeared. So he left and made his home here…because he doesn’t like change. He says that the drow knew Rook back when they lived in Dunduar. She promises to be on the lookout for rubies.

Ky catches back up with Aury and Hap. Tells him he looks great in the new armor and suggests the Old Oak and Grapes for dinner. They walk there. As they walk in, they find it is Ladies Night. They had Ky a goblet and say as a representative from Lady T, the original member of the Vintner’s Guild. And others hand goblets to Aury and Hap and say “to a new future.” These people are trying to be happy, but are also sewing division…maybe. Others in the room have these same goblets. We get seated, paying a cover, and enjoy a fine meal.

Meanwhile, Ainslie and Og go to the Crimson Swan to meet with the Cloak of Defiance. This is the nicest place they have ever been. Lighting is perfect, candles give feeling of warmth, they hear music, smell of cherry BBQ, soft but rich fragrance. As they walk in, someone locks eyes like they were expecting you. They come up to them and welcome them, “right this way.” She waits and follows them. They go into a private dining room, no sharp angles. A lady dressed in dark leather stands and says recognized and rewarded and perhaps ready for more. Love me some EGD. They see a fantastic meal…exotic and upscale foods (you don’t want to eat too fast). She says I really hope you can prosper in this city. Og grabs food and she says ‘that is only the beginning.’ You see that Emberstran can eat honest folks alive, but not you. Og introduces himself, Ainslie does, and she says I am Selvara Nendris, but everyone calls me the Black Quill. You know my friend Torrick. They eat. She says you’ve seen what happens here, you have been around. My friend can speak on your behalf. As you fill your bellies, I’m asking you to join us…not as pawns, but as peers. And believe me the next chest you open won’t be so light. Og says you have put effort into making us official EGD…what exactly are you looking for us to do? She says she needs to clear the objectives with her authorities…and then will let them know. She is trying to establish a partnership to increase their efficiencies in the town. The two talk in their heads and agree with her that an alliance could be beneficial to both sides. She raises her glass “Shall the Rook topple.” They both raise their glasses as Ainslie says he is trying to come up with another chess-related pun…other than “down with the queen.” They realize that was a toast to “are you in or out” and they both agreed to be in. She says you did good…there is always a choice and there are certain ones that lead you to the correct future to insure that you are still standing. Glad you enjoyed the meal. Once Jerik gives the word, I’ll be in touch. I hope next time we will meet again as kin.

– The End of the Session –
The Chronicle of Emberstran · Chapter 13
13

Emberheart Tablet

In-World22nd of Talil, 1246 WhereVarious Locations of Emberstran PlayedOctober 23, 2025

We have become known in the town–heroes to some, problems to others.

At the Temple … Aury and Hap come out for breakfast and they hear someone enter “Aury - you here, I got something you should see.” Arnol - “I found this…I think this is tied to you” (to Hap) and sets it next to him. This is a round medallion of black obsidian hanging from a copper chain rope. “This was in their stash…I took it from them when they were gone. I think they took it from a grave they were robbing. Your dad was buried there, right?” It has a “G” on it. “You recognize it, don’t you?” The second Hap touches it, the brazier in the room flickers. It is not familiar to him, but the G does look like his family’s “G.” “It didn’t do that when I touched it,” says Arnol. Hap says it looks like something his daddy had….but the chain is what seems familiar. He realizes that Ainslielee wears a chain just like this, but isn’t sure about it. Wants to spend time with it.

Aury wants to figure out what is going on with Arnol (after hearing his tale of what he just described). Hap tries to pay him and he declines…Hap thinks Arnol is guilty. Aury repeats some of his words to him…and asks if there is anything Diverra should know. Arnol says he will take it up with his goddess. He won’t lie to Aury. Aury says he needs to go see the Mother. He asks if he can clean up or eat first. Hap tries to defend Arnol. Arnol says he didn’t tell because he was told not to. Word play between them all about what he should tell…and Aury lands on “break your promise if you are breaking our vows to our goddess.” Arnol says he didn’t do the bad things…killed people, robbed graves. Aury and he come up with “guilt by association” and he says he will talk to the Mother and probably shouldn’t go back (to the bad folks). Hap asks who “they” are and he says he can’t tell. They two offer to help Arnol…who now feels bad about ever associating with the bad people. He says he needs to think of this. Hap says he owes him since he did “him a solid” and will help if needed. Aury agrees…he waves his hand and magically cleans up Arnol. Hap is excited to sit and reflect and study his new medallion.

Meanwhile, at the Warm Bear, Ainslie and Og wake up and discuss their day yesterday. Getting the tablet piece, their dinner with the Cloak. They think Aury is 50/50, Hap and Ky dislike the Cloak…but now that they are “employed” by them…what does that mean. Are they Cloak agents or maybe Double Agents? They discuss how Og is not great at lying, but Ainslie can lie for them. Ainslie thinks that maybe they should convince Rook to leave town; Og says they could via burning down the vineyards or the town. They talk about letting Rook know they (the Cloak) want to get rid of her. But at a minimum, decide to meet the party at the Temple to show them the tablet piece they acquired. Will avoid the Cloak talk with the party unless it comes up. Og brings up that Ky may talk to Rook about all of this. They agree that they don’t want to lie to the party. They decide that they will share their interaction with the Cloak, but that they don’t agree with the Cloak’s goals. They decide to hit up the gertot leg vendor on the way to the Temple.

As soon as they walk downstairs, everything seems still and quiet with Og. He feels a motion as if something is crawling around in his backpack. He stops to go through his pack and a vine of the thornleaf grips onto Og and to the inside of his wrist and he feels a thorn prick him and blood is drawn. It grasps like a tick momentarily and he feels like his skin is changing (lizard shedding skin) and in his mind he hears a soft sound (old tree gets pushed by the wind and you hear creaking timbers) and then it forms the word “Alferumn” (a couple of times). He reaches down and it releases from his wrist, and it goes back into pendant form. He has a feeling that it was asking for something that he didn’t answer. When he looks at where the thorn was, he now sees that it is like tree bark on his wrist. Og tells Ainslie about what just happened and the word that he heard…Ainslie tries to see if he knows this word, and he does. He thinks back to stories he’s heard when he was captured by the cult and he doesn’t think the name has anything to do with the cult, but more of a name that he heard from that group about someone who spoke about an axis of perpetualism…like the circle of life everywhere it sits. He thinks it has something to do with the Ascension Tree (tree of life in Myrdae). Maybe this leaf is from that tree, thinks Og (who found it in a cave). Og puts it on his wrist and it latches on again…but he can tell this doesn’t want to hurt him…it is almost asking for permission to stay on him. Og talks to it “hello, Mr. Leaf, can you hear me?” And it grips tighter. He says he will talk to it later, after he eats. Ainslie tells him that he needs to feed the leaf, too.

Meanwhile, at the Hydra: Ky wakes with a sharp knock at her door and Tessa comes in, uninvited. She is damp and looks tired “we need to talk. Tell me your friends did not have dinner with the Cloak.” Ky says she doesn’t know what’s going on, but that they probably have a good reason. They have a discussion about how someone needs to change - them or Ky. Ky tells her about the EGD and how she asked the Lord to give them official standing. Ky says perhaps they can be double agents. Tessa says that if we need the guys to go before Belavie, we can do that. But that our group is too well known to be in two different opposing groups. She apologizes for barging in…and notes that her skills to do so were good. She leaves and Ky leaves, heading out without eating, to get to the Temple.

It is misty as the groups walk to the Temple to meet. Ainslie notices a torn parchment hanging on a lamppost “by decree of Lord Dusklight…new stewardship for the VG will be happening soon. Lady T will be reappointed or revoked…this will take part at a Council meeting next stretch.” They get the sense that this doesn’t happen often. Ainslie tells Og. They arrive at the Temple. They see Arnol sitting with Aury and Hap…and see Ky arriving at the other side. Arnol says, “I should probably go speak to her” and walks away.”

Ky says “what happened last night?” rather loudly. They gather closer and she says I heard you had dinner with the Cloak. Ky questions what the Cloak’s goals are and why they asked them to dinner. She tells them that they cannot be Cloak members or be seen to be Cloak members…unless they are secretly getting information from the Cloak to feed Ky. The party talks about the politics of the situation - who to align with, etc., as Ainslie shares what he learned about the meeting that has been called for the next stretch. Aury thinks through his experiences as a resident of the town…and thinks the Council aligns 3 (non-cloak) vs 2 (cloak focused)...and that the Lord leans toward the side of the 2/Cloak. He’s not sure how the vote next week will go. Group talks about how we could tip the scales with our influence, but Ky points out that we cannot be both Etterset and Cloak. Ky mentions how they could be double agents. Ainslie and Og aren’t sure they could pull that off.

Ainslie ends up changing the subject and shows the tablet piece they found. They tell the other three that it was found with the cultists. Arnol makes a noise and Aury thinks in his head that he might be tied somehow to the retrieval. We need to go to a safe space to put it together. Decide to go to the Dyed Cane to have a safe and private room to assemble the tablet.

We head to the DC and see if we can find Bordi. He says he will send up the tea and some food and throws us the key to the room. Aury says he needs the brandy. We go up to the private room. As we walk up, Hap shows Ainslie the chain/medallion and he knows they have chains that are just alike. He thinks the onyx medallion looks like it has magic drained from it, but not broken…as if it could be restored. Like it needs to be recharged. Ainslie now thinks that his chain is missing a pendant or medallion. Ainslie tells Hap about it…and we discuss how Hap got his from Arnol (and tells the story). Discuss how maybe the two chains came from the same source. Ky suggests a trip to the jeweler in town.

We lock the door after the food/drinks are dropped off. Hap suggests getting the tablet pieces out - and we all do. As we do, we talk about the cultists…Og says they were digging graves. Aury asks if they saw Arnol there at the site…and they said they did not see him while they were getting the tablet piece. Og even suggests that Aury should talk to Arnol about the cult group.

We push the pieces of the tablet together and nothing happens….then the rooms feels of energy and the tablet vibrates. Orange light traces a sigil on the surface…edges glow. Starts to smell like burning. Warmth coming from tablet. The runes then set into place…can any of us read it? None of us can. Ky does a rubbing on it and feels the arcane magic. Hap tries to copy it. Ainslie casts a spell to see what the magic of the tablet is: transmutation, divination, and illusion. The tablet is roughly 6-7” by 8”. Ky feels like the magic is starting to enter her…and she stops and hands it to Aury. Ainslie suggests that Ky ask Rook (Im) for a scroll of Identification to see if she can give it to her. Aury gives it to Hap to hold.

Group talks about next steps. Ainslie mentions that Hap can wear his necklace that hides him from being scryed upon (and gives it to Hap). All think it is a good idea. Ky says she will go visit Rook. Og offers to go with her, but since he has a stone that Rook wants, will stay back. Discuss who else should go. Ainslie will go with Ky. She will carry the stone and will leave the coil behind for someone to listen in. Aury, Og, and Hap will stay behind (and plan to stay at least an hour).

As we sit there, we notice that Og’s skin is starting to look strange. As he tells us the story, the appearance of the bark disappears. He is now one with the bracelet (+1 to armor class).

Ky and Ainslie head to Im’s Tower. 20 minutes later they arrive at the Tower. Ky knocks on the door. Usual arrival. We are escorted to the dining area. Ky starts to question Rook about Befast and the gem operation and reveals that she is an Umberfist and was sent out from Dunduar to find out who has been stealing from her clan. Rook gets defensive and upset with the accusations…Ky didn’t mean to get loud and upset…but did. They smooth it out as Rook offers to help her investigate who might be stealing from her clan. She suggests it might be the Cragfury clan. She says that she is helping Befast and the dark gnomes make a life for themselves outside of the underdark. She asks for Ky’s support and Ky says she has it. She then asks Ainslie for his support. Ainslie then tells Rook about how the EGD was not sanctioned from the Lord, but from the Cloak. He asks in a round about way for an identification scroll. He then asks Rook about the Order of Ahn. She has heard of it and says that if she could provide him with information about it or the Ahn’tsuel - she will have his 100% support. She asks for Ky to be escorted to her quarters to get the Scroll of Identify. Ky goes.

Rook asks Ainslie why he is interested in Ahn and he said he might be getting his power from there. He shows her his tattoos. She talks to him about a dragon that used to fly over Emberstran named Irragosa. He came from the same hatch as Annagos and was the mount for one of the descendants of Ahn’tsuel. Irragosa will have answers for you. She will be at Mount Emberstran. Rook has not seen her for some time. Ainslie then tells her about how the Cloak wants to take her down, by recruiting Ainslie and Og. She asks about the nature of their pact/contract. And she’s asking what would happen if he betrays their dinner. She is now concerned that he is bound by the Cloak.

Ky reenters with the scroll. They talk about the Cloak and how Ky wants them to be double agents. Rook presses that she will lobby for her support to her friends. Rook asks how she can help him out of this situation and he says he has it handled.

Back in the room, the group heard a lot of crackling…and only part of the conversation. During the 1 hour and 10 minutes Ky and Ainslie were gone, the other 3 were in the room with the tablet on the table. Aury starts to feel warmth and the runes on the tablet are starting to give an orange glow (molten lava). He feels grounded like something is calling him into the world of Myrdae. He notices that he feels more connected to Emberstran than he ever has before. He feels like it is coming from the mountain, the volcano, the heat of the mountain and the energy there. The tablet attuned to Aury during that period.

Aury can now read the tablet. It is a poem.

When he finishes reading it, everything around him goes quiet as he goes into the zone. He feels the heartbeat of the mountain. He feels the warmth pull at him and it calls you towards Mount Emberstran and he feels like it would lead him to a particular spot there. Hap and Og notice and ask what is up. He says “we have to go to the mountain - there is something there.” He doesn’t know why…just knows he is being called there.

Ky and Ainslie come back. We share that Ainslie and Og are not going to be Cloak. Aury tells the other two that he attuned to the tablet and about the poem and that we now need to go to the mountain. Ky asks Aury which is stronger - the pull to Diverra’s portal or the mountain. And Ainslie answers “the mountain.” Ky realizes that Ainslie has a strong personal reason for going to the mountain. Hap argues that his vision is what brought him to Emberstran and brought our group together…and thinks that it was tied to Diverra. Og feels that the portal is more risky because coming back might be hard, but also leans that way because of his initial vision, too. Ky says she will go that way, but recommends that we endorse Rook before we go. Aury and Hap agree…as do Ainslie and Og.

Ky suggests that we meet at the gate; Aury says out of town is better. We discuss and the group decides to meet at the Lord’s Keep.

At the Warm Bear Inn

Og and Ainslie head to the Bear to collect their things (papers about Hap, gemstones, etc.). A half-elf female bumps into them. They don’t know her but she is striking. She bumped into them on purpose. She looks dark-skinned, blue eyes, red cloak. “I was sent to see how things are going.” Og says they are going out of town. She asks if they are aware of the meeting next stretch...and they are. She asks how she can secure their support from the EGD against Rook. Ainslie says it would be in the best interest of the EGD to stay out of politics in order to keep order established. And to prove that they are completely impartial, he returns the money that they were given. She declines and says she is not going to take their money from them. They ask if she is going to report to the Quill. They get the sense that this person is probably here to take care of bigger things. She isn’t going to do any reporting, but was sent here for other tasks. She says she is here to ask the EGD to help solve the mystery of who killed the Cloak members in the sewers. She says she would like to tell Jerik when they will return from out of town. Og thinks it would be no more than a stretch. She says that might not be good, but leaves (this is Silverheart).

At the Temple to Diverra

Aury and Hap go to collect their things at the Temple. Aury tells the Priestess that they are heading to the Portal. Aury bows to her. She talks about how Arnol is troubled. She asks if he has heard about the grave diggers. Aury tells her that he thinks that Arnol won’t reveal everything because he thinks his head will explode. She asks Aury to take him under his wing. He says that he can’t because he is going to the portal. “Ahhhh….Diverra’s work.” Aury offers suggestions for Arnol - make sure Arnol reevaluates his vows. He says to research the head exploding thing. And that he might be involved in a “cult outside of us.” She senses that calling in him to leave…and takes away the task to take over Arnol. Aury mentions the vault and asks if there is anything magical there that Hap can wield. She offers to fetch him the Great Magical Pruning Dagger of Diverra (+1 dagger)...and goes to get it. Aury gives that to Hap. She also gives him a vial of holy water.

At the Headless Hydra

Ky leaves to meet with Tessa, then goes to the Hydra to get her stuff. Ky tells her that we will support Rook; that Ainslie and Og will be double agents. They can’t openly betray the Cloak. Tell her that we are leaving town and aren’t sure when we will be back. Aury is listening in…but this is happening while he is also talking to the Priestess about Arnol, the magical weapon, the call to the portal, etc.

Back to Og and Ainslie - Ainslie tells Og that he will need to go to the Mountain at some point - that he has personal reasons. They discuss the visit from Silverheart. They discuss needing to remain neutral. Ainslie casts sending to Rook…tells her that EGD needs to be apolitical. She responds to Ainslie: that is unacceptable, I have already engaged. Jerik’s brother is held in your defense. (I have Phira’s husband…he is a hostage…if they make a move on you, Jerik’s brother will be killed).

– The End of the Session –

I have found her to be intelligent and someone who is ready to help take Emberstran to the next level. Drowned Waypoint

In-World22nd of Talil, 1246 WhereVarious Locations of Emberstran PlayedNovember 12, 2025

Ky heads to the Lord’s Keep; Og and Ainslie heading to the Gate from the Bear; Aury and Hap at the Temple, about to head to the Gate.
