The Lower Baltwood Forest

We pass people here and there and the road fades behind us. We find ourselves on a familiar trail leading to the grove. The woods are humming with light, better than they were last we were here. We see movement on the trail. There is someone crouched by a fallen log - it is someone who knows us. It is a hunter from the Warm Bear Inn. Ainslie thinks he is sincere. The guy says he has not seen anyone go into the heart of the grove since the blight cleared, but if the heroes are going in, it must be ok. Ainslie gives him 1gp to drink on their behalf at the Warm Bear Inn. Ky asks his name…it was Kale Noven (a storyteller about hunting the golden stag).

We follow trails and cross the creek…starting to get darker. As we enter the grove, there is a calmness. We hear a crack of branches and a guttural growl. We see the animal that we avoided last time.

IT’S INITIATIVE: Combat vs. Dralg (beast)

  • ROUND ONE: Ainslie recognizes the creature, casts animal handling and steps forward, pulling Og closer to him. The creature (dralg) steps forward and stops in front of Ainslie. Hap steps forward and casts a defensive spell. Ky moves up and draws her crossbow, holding her action (that’s a good beastie). Og moves toward Ky and has his weapon ready. Aury stays put. Three more dralg step forward and growl - looking menacing.
  • ROUND TWO: Ainslie pulls out rations and steps forward with food. The OG dralg walks up to Ainslie and starts to eat rations. Hap looks at the creatures to see who might be hostile - but isn’t sure what the situation is. He moves up to ready an attack. Ky moves up - heading toward the area with the portal - crossbow still at the ready. Og steps up and gives them rations…and then moves next to Ky. Aury moves forward a little bit. The two other dralgs run up to the rations and starts to eat. The last one eases up and starts to eat also.

We are out of combat. We travel through the grove and make it to the pedestal. The stone starts to rise up about 60’ and is aligned with the stone face adjacent to the cliff face. Sun is setting - fantastic view. Aury feels like this lift would not work unless there is a worshipper of Diverra on it. He thinks that this waypoint would not work without having a Diverra follower in the group (has a divine restriction). We decide to camp here at this cave and go through the waypoint in the morning.

Ky perceives that the cave is safe - just like we left it. Og doesn’t see any tracks other than ours - thinks no one has been here since we were last here. Og falls asleep. Ainslie decides to message Phira on Hap’s behalf before we retire for the night. They go back and forth several times. Phira says Rook is good and that she is protecting her husband because of his troubled past. Ainslie thanks her and wishes her well. Group takes a long rest for the night.

Ainslie reaches out to Arnol and sends another sending spell…telling him that he is on the right path, one of his most loyal followers (trying to mimic a lich). Fleur is on watch…as the group rests.

Arcane Waypoint for Diverra’s Faithful

We wake up to a beautiful sunrise. This is what you’ve been waiting for, Aury. We stand in the circle. Aury casts a spell for the first time - to activate the sigil. He is stumbling a little, but then feels warmth and pulls himself together. We gather close…and the light changes. We feel like we haven’t moved, but yet the cave walls melt away. They come back as if they are liquid. The light flickers. We see something move by us. We realize that we are in a protected dome under the sea. We see the opening in the dome leads into a cave. This symbol is correct and is what was on the wall. We think we might about 100’ under water. Ky puts her hand through the membrane - it passes through and gets wet. She suggests going into the cave.

Underwater Cave

Og looks for tracks and sees enormous tracks that have been here a stretch or two - he thinks the circle hasn’t been used in 20 years. We think this circle hasn’t been used since the Awakening…and that 20 years ago this place might not have been under water. Aury knows that a sanctuary to Diverra exists historically - he just doesn’t know where. He thinks this is at a “hub” location.

Ky stealths into the cave (27). It slopes downward…feels seawater seeping through the walls. She doesn’t leave footprints…communicates with Ainslie what is going on. After traveling for some time, the path winds; after 30 minutes, we are probably another 150’ down. Aury casts light on Og’s weapon to help light the passage. Eventually a smell starts to emerge…spoiled meat. It opens into a large cavern. There are hundreds of bones. There is a hulking figure with mottled green skin with long arms and a nose. These are the tracks we spotted in the bubble. Ky comes back to stop the group and describes it. Hap thinks this is a troll.

Troll Cavern

We walk into the cavern. Og gets big. Ainslie speaks in giant with a menacing voice. They say there is no need to fight. Ainslie tries to persuade the trolls to let us go into the pool - which he says is his potty place. There are 3. They say “maybe” and step back. They continue to eat and watch our group. Ky steps forward and Og picks her up and she goes limp (like food). Hap walks in and Ainslie picks him up and licks him. Og then picks up Aury and tries to lick him and Aury slaps Og and he says (in giant) I like my food spicy. The trolls laugh. Aury looks around - this place smells horribly - he notices a fishing net with some items in it (pouch, clay pot, etc.). There doesn’t seem to be another passage - just the pool. Og takes Aury and Ky and steps into the pool…they all hold their breath and start to swim. Ainslie notices a piece of paper next to the water and grabs it. Hap and Ainslie step into the pool and hold their breath, too. Not long after swimming, about 10’ down and 7’...it opens into a huge body of water. We surface on the water…there is water as far as you can see. The ceiling is higher than 120’ at some points.

We think this could almost be a passage into the underdark. We see a ledge - that would be a path. We all get on to the ledge. The piece of paper that Ainslie grabbed: “lights under the water at night, moving like roots.” Ainslie pockets it. We all get dry and start to walk on the ledge.

Shenog the Fisher

We follow the path and it narrows. Sounds are muffled and echoed here…almost distorted. Even dripping water sounds odd. We see small ripples in the water below us. The area is dimly lit by lichen. We see a glowing lantern around the bend and see something that looks like a boat. This seems like a boat in a raft. We see a figure standing on the side of the bank. We keep going and hear “careful there, the bank drops off fast.” The big woman with red braided hair and gray skin and yellow glowing eyes starts to pole the boat our way. She grabs the lantern and pulls it out front as she nears us. She has a leather jerkin. “Shenog - been fishing this lake for a long time. You travelers look lost.” Aury says “I”m a priest of Diverra and looking for our temple…maybe a grove…something that will lead me to my goddess.” She doesn’t seem to recognize what he is saying. “Is there a place with buildings or ruins.” She knows of some. Says they are in Nunglthil. She is just a big half-orc. Ky says “Shenog - is that your name” She says “was i not clear.” Ainslie asks why she is here - I live down here and trade in Nunglthil. Og says “where do you live” and she turns and looks at her boat. Ainslie asks about the dangers in the waters. Do you not know these waters? Welcome to Rothenloch. What are the dangers - some would say the hums at night get louder. Ainslie asks about what he saw on the paper. Are there moving roots in the water and she says yes. They ask about how to get to the surface and she knows how. Ky asks her in orcish about what she trades (riches, magic, etc.). There is some discussion about the fare to take the group on her boat to Nunglthil. Ky (in orcish) agrees to pay her 6gp for the group - after some intimidation.

It will take an hour or two to paddle to the hamlet. On route, Ainslie reads a book to the group. Og gets tired after an hour and Hap takes over. She pulls out some smoked eel to share with the group. Ainslie asks her about thieves guilds…and she confirms that the Cloak is in the town. Mostly there are quaggoths in this area (large humanoid creatures). Ainslie starts to tell her about Emberstran. She then tells the group about the Shade of Rothenloch…it takes people’s minds and they never return the same, if it all. The eel was tasty. We finally start to see flickering lights in the distance. We start to see a wall come into view. There is an outpost that is half carved into the stone. Houses lit by blue-ish light. “Best to keep your eyes on your purses here.” She pulls up to the docks. This seems very shady.

We think this will be an interesting place to seek answers - about Diverra, about the underdark, about where we actually are.

– The End of the Session –

Spitespirit Cache

24th of Talil - Darkbottom Draught, Nunglthil

Next morning we go down to meet Zymve…we see there are new boats in the harbor, unloading boats (across the cove). Zymve tells us we have to work our way to the other side to meet Scalamank. She points out and we start to get a good sense of the people in the various enclaves. When we get to the other side, we go into his shop “Coppereyes, Gemcraft”…there is a “bell” of a soft ding and a flash of light. There are walls lined with merchandise and a counter. We see a gnome with copper eyes. Great lighting. He acknowledges Zymve…she says “we are here about the key.” He locks the doors…you think they can manage it? She says the others haven’t been able to do it. He taps he belt. I’ve paid good money for the third key and I’m looking to get some gems from Mandel Spitespirit. Everyone may have heard a tale of a guy named Spitespirit. He says that the guy hid everything in the Spitespirit cache…I’m looking to get all of the uncut gems. We ask him why not go yourself…and he says that others went into other rooms with other keys and never returned. I just need someone to get the one chest of uncut gems and you can have anything else you find. Is this a goosechase? He says he will give us the key for 100gp. If we return, we get the 100gp back, plus another 100gp and any additional loot. Ainslie renegotiates - says instead, just give us a 300gp gem when we return.

Discussion. We agree. Ainslie fronts the money. He gives us the key and tells us where to go. Path, stone staircase, room. Will be a half day’s travel. We realize that we won’t get a fair deal on trades until we establish our reputation.

We travel there and see a large room with a heavy iron portcullis there with a passage beyond that leads further to the surface. We see a huge guy - Glayig - who is a guard to this passage. He lets us through. We enter the tunnel and travel…rest every now and then. We finally come to where a rock fall area was cleared. The travel has been dark, but group used light to see. We come to the area where it was rumored that people haven’t come back from. Aury thinks this isn’t totally true.

Ky scouts ahead of the group down the spiral staircase. It opens into a lantern-lit chamber. There are 12 walls, as described. There is fungus in the lantern to illuminate the room. 2 of the 12 walls have arched open passages that lead on. The rest of the walls have keyholes. We look at the pictures on the walls: mage summoning, traveler making deal with a devil, etc. One stands out and shows a xorn showing gems. We think this is the right one. Aury asks about picking locks for the one we don’t have keys for and Ainslie thinks they are all warded. Ainslie tries to think about how a xorn might have been associated with the guy from before and he doesn’t. The walls with open arches have no clues. Ky tells the group that xorn’s eat gems.

Ky steps up to investigate the door. She doesn’t see anything mechanical…very strange. It is stone - looks like it would be perfectly suited for a skeleton key. Ainslie uses mage hand to open the lock and it glows and then fades to dust. Then the ancient mechanism begins to work…and the walls slide down revealing the archway. Cool air, earthy, metallic, oily…

Straight hallway - 10’ wide x 10’ tall passage, runes carved every 10’, goes straight for some distance. Pillar inside. After about 50’ the height of the ceiling reduces to 2’ off the ground (or so it seems). Ky and Og can read the runes. It seems to be proper names…don’t know if it is places or people. Think it is likely people. Ky thinks the names might be from old tales, but nothing that stands out. As we near 30’ of the pillar, Ainslie (with his detect magic still up) senses magic from the pillar. Ky bends down to check out the pillar and sees that there are large cracks in the wall…the pillar is holding up the wall. And the passage continues at the 10’ beyond. But the cracks in the walls are significant…and a collapse could be significant and kills us. Ky asks Og to lift her up to investigate the cracks…looking for a triggering mechanism. She thinks there is a 10x10x8 boulder cube here blocking the way. Ky looks and sees a button on the rod (silver, horses on it). Ainslie tries to use mage hand on the button and it didn’t work. Hap tries to send Fleur down to push the button. We hear a click and the groan of rock and then a pop…and Fleur is crushed.

Og lifts Ky up and she starts to crawl. She sees that the top is still insecure. Hap gives her his shield and she uses the rod (which was pushed out the other side) and shield to reinforce the center of the new ceiling. Aury casts guidance on everyone; Zymve casts dancing light so everyone can see. Ainslie starts to come across the top - makes it with no problem. Zymve tries and loosens some stones and takes damage. Og comes across with no issues. Hap comes across with no issues. Aury is the last to try and he dislodges some stones as well, taking damage. Ky goes back up to get the rod and Hap’s shield. Ky keeps the rod.

We work our way down the passage and it bends to the left. A slope down starts to happen. Ky notices it is a steep grade, and Og feels hit foot start to slide…and the hall suddenly lights up by candles and we see muck on the ground, very oily. It is slick and Og and Ky both make saving throws to keep from falling. Ky points this out…and Aury immediately grabs his shield to slide down the incline. Og and Ky are blown back…dex saves. Aury caused the oil to light up and slid through a tunnel of flame. Og and Ky take damage from the explosion. Aury sees a plain wall with a handprint in the middle of it at the bottom. Og and Ainslie try to dampen the candle wicks on the walls as the flames die down. Ky points out the trigger to the light. Hap slides down on his shield. Og slides down on the saddlebag. Ainslie slides down on a cloak. Zymve slides down on a bedroll, as does Ky. Everything we slid on is oily and dirty. Ainslie and Aury clean everything with their spells.

There is a human-shaped handprint on the wall. Ky investigates the wall to make sure that there are no other traps or anything else. The room is 20’ long hall ending in a 10’x10’ room with the handprint on the wall. Ky notices that the two last runes had the same name Ythibius. Ainslie and Zymve think that sounds like a dragon’s name. Ainslie puts his hand on the handprint. The stone behind it clicks and the wall collapses as if it is an illusion. We see a small rope bridge that goes out to a plateau that has steps that go down then that goes to a dais where the ray of light hits (ray of violet light shining down) onto an ornate box. There are other coins and trinkets on the dais. Aury thinks the bridge could support all of us. Ky starts across by herself, investigating as she goes. She feels as if the ceiling appears like a night sky. About halfway across, Og starts to walk. When Ky gets to the plateau, a vast shape appears above…two large wings form from the mist. She says - Ythibius is that you? IT IS INITIATIVE.

Round One: The dragon goes first and the group must take a dex saving throw - everyone takes necrotic damage. Hellish rebuke from Hap. Ky attacks with her crossbow and crits…hard. The dragon then rears back and Ky takes damage and he disappears into the shadows. Ainslie runs up and casts a spell on Og (haste) and then runs next to Kytha. The dragon then lands next to Ky and attacks Ky with rend and misses. Aury heals Ky for 21 pts. He looks briefly at Aury and then looks at Ainslie and pounces on him, doing damage. Og hits the dragon and then crits him, lots of damage. Then runs away and the dragon gets an opportunity attack and hits. The dragon pounces down next to Og…and Ky misses her attack of opportunity. Hap firebolts the dragon - great damage. And will cast second wind on himself. Zymve whips out her yarting (instrument) and casts dissonant whispers and saves but still takes psychic damage. Then uses her beguiling magic but nothing else happens. And she gives herself a bardic inspiration.

Round Two: At the start of the dragon’s turn, vines emerge from Og and he has to make a strength saving throw. The dragon then attacks Og…and hits a couple of times. Ky dashes to the side of the dias to hide, holding her action that if he comes into view she will attack. He then shows up and pounces on Og and Ky hits with her crossbow. The dragon then hits Og, critically. Ainslie shoots lightning at the dragon and he fails, this kills him. He vanishes in a puff of smoke and the door behind the group opens again.

Aury tells the group not to touch anything for 10 minutes…while he prays over us (all but Hap get healed, benefit of short rest). Ky goes up to the dais. There is a box, an ebon blade with draconic runes (short sword named shadow trail - Hap takes this), small chip of a violet prism shard with a few affixed to the ceiling (creates light cantrip in violet light - Ainslie takes it), medallion with markings (Mandel’s Adventuring medallion - might be a group the guy was in - value of 35gp for the metal, more if you find a collector); bone scrollcase; ring with inscription - “luck is for the living” (non magical - worth 80gp); 14pp (Ainslie takes 10); 112gp; 287sp. Lock on box has abjuration magic; scrolls of feather fall (Ainslie) and pass without trace (Zymve). Sword is very magical…words come into Ainslie head.

Everyone moves away from the box since we think it is trapped. Ainslie steps forward and tries to open the box. There is no discernable way to open it. He mage hands the box to raise it up and sees a key. He uses the key and the box opens. Inside we see 14 very large uncut gems - Zymve and Ky think these are worth minimum 250gp/each. We discuss that maybe we need a better deal. Hap thinks the blade is worth much more than the gems.

We figure our way back to the room with 12 walls. We decide to look at the two open archway rooms and there is no opening in either one to get through, assuming that the passages continue. We now have a key, however, to the box, so we know what keys look like that would be useful for this area.

We make our way back to the guard at the portcullis - a day has passed. We are now walking back into the town, ready to talk to the guy with a box. We get rooms at the same place. Zymve decides to stay with Hap and Aury. We sleep here and the next day plan to go to Coppereyes. Hap plans to study the sword at bedtime.

– The End of the Session –

Favor in Nunglthil

In-World25th of Talil, 1246 WhereNunglthil PlayedJanuary 7, 2026

25th of Talil in the Darkbottom Draught

We rest at the Darkbottom Draught…a little hard to discern that it is morning being down here. Will take some getting used to, but smell scents of cooking which helps us know it is breakfast. A lanky ulbin comes over and shows us places to sit - almost as if he was looking for us. There is a sealed letter for us there. Ky picks it up and it is from Zymve…she has unfinished business and she asks us to tell Coppereyes she will see him later. Ains asks how everyone slept…no issues. The ulbin, Tickrin, comes by to serve us - breakfast and an ale for Aury and Og. Aury offers a blessing to him.

Ky notices someone sitting behind Ains. It is Endavo sitting alone. He has a small book. She asks the group if we should invite him over to join us and they concur. Ains messages him and he says he will be over after he finishes the chapter. As we wait, Ains asks Hap about his sword. He says he has never seen anything like this before. It talks in his head. We all see the sword vibrate in the sheath next to Hap. Ky asks if it is good or bad? He says he doesn’t know. Og asks about a name and Hap replies “Shadowtrail.” Hap says “You don’t get this type of sword in the swamp, but of course, I already have the pruning dagger of Diverra. I’m not swimming in riches, but now have two powerful weapons.” Ains comments that the most powerful weapon is “in your head.” Then he excuses himself to find a restroom.

Endavo closes his book and stands up to join our group. The book says “Misplaced a Last Time.” He asks how we like Nunglthil. We comment - it is a revelation, taking time to acclimate, etc. We ask him about his time…and he comments that he has been enjoying Miss Placed Tomes. Dririel is the owner of this bookstore (Serlaeth Enclave) and he writes books. This is one of a series of 4 books he has written. He asks if we have been on the lake? Aury replies yes - and that we’ve seen turds. Ky explains that we had a guide coming in. Endavo says there is a beast in the lake that “hunts for teeth” - speaks none, listens all, remembers, doesn’t use words to speak, fisherman vanish, dreams change, those that hear the calls too clearly are not the same. She’nog, our guide, didn’t mention this to us - says Hap. Aury asks if this is a Siren? He thinks not. Ky asks if it dwells in the depths. He says yes. Aury asks if there are other cities on this lake and he says we should visit the mapmaker - he hears there is a drow city on this lake. Ky asks if this is his first time here and he replies it is “his second time.” She notices that he is wearing something he wasn’t wearing in Emberstran - it looks to be a leather strap around his neck with different beads, different varieties. As she notices this, Endavo comments on Hap’s sword and Hap tells him a shortened version of how he acquired it.

Og asks Endavo where we should go or visit while in this town. He asks why we are here and Og says we are here for the priest. Endavo comments on the story Aury told last time. Aury said he’d tell a new tale, but instead tells him we are looking for an old temple to Diverra. He advises we see the mapmaker, maybe visit Miss Placed Tomes, but doesn’t think there is a temple in this area. He tells us the Ascending Path leads to the Wurmrise - an odd name - but this is an ascending rock that spirals up to the surface. It is an odd shape. Ky asks how he got down here? He says that is a tale for another day, but that he got a favor from Lady Rook to get there. At that point, he says he needs to tend to other business. Ky asks how long he will be in town and he says “fate will tell.”

We decide to go visit Coppereyes and discuss that we will all abide by the agreement we made. All agree. We step out to the harbor area. We see Gral’thune folks walking around (dwarves and ulbin)...we see strangers wearing earrings, bracelets, necklaces, charms on backpacks, etc. with beads - and think this is tied to favor.

Visiting Coppereyes

We open the door to the gemshop and there is a chime that we remember. Just like before, Scalamank (Coppereyes) comes forward. Ah - you are back…and successful? He says to show him the box of gems. And we give him the key. He opens it. Ah - look at this, as he starts to finger the gems - “exactly as accounted for.” We think he is sincere. He says, let me get you a 300gp diamond. Ains also asks for the lockbox as well. He mentions that we did him a favor…and gives us each a luminescent bead (this shows us that we have favor with Glimmerdel). And tells us that we need to display our favor - recommends that Ky displays hers in her hair. Og picks up the lockbox; Ains takes the key. He gives Ains a 325gp diamond. He looks at Ky and points to the half broken charm around her neck - and says he wouldn’t wear that. He starts to multitask - mentions Szoradryn - and is triggered. Who am I to judge people with their ways? If you can find out from Szoradryn what he would actually wear, that would help straighten things out between us. He made something for Szoradryn and he was insulted by how Szoradryn treated him/the thing he made. Ky buys a 5gp chain from him that she puts in her hair.

Aury asks if he can affix the bead to his scepter. He says he can make a band for it, but it will be 10gp. He says I will work on it. Ky asks where we can find Szoradryn - and he replies that he runs Silken Secrets. We wait on him to finish the band. Ains asks if he is willing to purchase an onyx box from him. He considers it. He asks how much Ains wants for it and he says 125gp; Ains then says that he will discount it to 120gp and he agrees to buy it.

Og tries to communicate with his leaf and gets no response. But he feels the vine twist and he’s able to affix his bead to it. Ains decides that he will use his mage hand spell to have it circle his head continuously. Hap will figure out another way to display his bead.

We leave the shop and discuss where to go. Ains suggests that we split up. We all go to the mapmaker, passing by the Glimmerdel Center (as we realize that each group has their own “headquarters”).

Paths by Stonesniff

We go into Paths by Stonesniff. We see curios on display by the door. We see something large with many legs hanging that casts a shadow (a giant stuffed spider). There is a large mushroom shaped table in the middle of the room. There are many lockboxes on one side of the room. Room spells like old parchment. This is a hoarder-type place vs. a shop. Display case made out of umberhulk shell. A stocky, deep gnome runs the place, braided beard w/copper wire - this is Bolshack Stonesniff. Maps - that’s why everyone is here. Understand this - the underdark doesn’t stay put, creatures dig, a guide keeps you alive not my maps. Still better to know where you are going that go in blind. Hap banters with him - “so we know where we are going to die.” “You get it” and they laugh together. Ains asks what is the point of maps if everything is constantly changing. But he says he has to do this. He mentions that if anyone ever has anything that I don’t have that I can display, I’ll give you favor. Ky immediately shows him her Mummified Otherworldly Tadpole and he loves it and agrees to trade her a bead of Glimmerdel for it. They do.

Aury then talks to him about the temple of Diverra that he is looking for. He pulls out a map - green-ish gray hide. He shows us where we are on his “Map of the Underdark.” We don’t see anything on this map that looks like it could be where the Sanctuary of Diverra is, but Aury looks at the map and thinks that we likely came in between Thallgrove and Nunglthil. He then shows us another map that shows us Rothenloch on it ... and we think we need to find a place on the eastern most edge of the Thallgrove area. He explains that the red areas on his map are classically evil; gold would be fine; green would be accepting of most. Ky asks about Thallgrove and he says that you go mad there (from the spores and fungi). He says the genling is a little creature who was affected by the bloodwars - descendent of the myconid. He hired a genling to be a scout to help create his map. Ky asks if we can earn favor by finding out new geographical features, and he says he will pay favor for any corrections to his maps. She asks if we can copy the maps and he says that we can - so we do. We gather that Nunglthil is “below” Hap’s home town - the Ahndashere.

The group discusses the maps of the Underdark and the surface. Aury’s gut tells him the Sanctuary was lost and no one has seen it since the Awakening…why the question is why? Only one way in/out? Some reason no one comes back? We thank him for his time and leave. Have the sense that he could recommend a guide if needed.

Og decides to head to Soothing Spores and sees a father/son setting up stalls. He notices the docks and boats heading in and out of the area, and people unloading and going to shops and stalls.

Miss Placed Tomes

The rest of the group heads to Miss Placed Tomes to see if we can get any additional information. Inside, the walls are thick and the place is quiet. There is an out of tune chime as we enter. Tall, narrow shop…there is a smell of brewing tea. Shelves and books everywhere and a lofted area above with alcoves/reading room. A silver-haired drow walks over - this is Dririel. Ains asks if he has a book on Spitespirits and he asks if we have been dealing with Coppereyes. He says he does not have a book about that, but would put out a finder on it. He says he will bring over a book that Coppereyes read for Ains to read. Ains and he discuss that he has been behind a door…and then he reveals that someone borrowed a book that has some information on the murals that show what “could” be behind other doors and he will let Ains borrow it in a stretch. Aury asks about a book on the missing Sanctuary of Diverra….does he have one? Books about love and beauty - and jokes with him about where he is staying. He says he will find books about historical references and also travelers journals that might help - and points to an alcove that Aury can use to do research. Ains then asks about research on cults and he says he has some materials and likes that Ains is mysterious. He jokes about where Ains is staying. We go to the alcove and Aury pulls out his calligraphy kit to impress him (he shouts no transcription - just notes). He thinks Aury has good handwriting.

Ains goes to the cult section and it is easy to find…he discovers that the group/person he is studying is crazy (off their rocker). He reads between the lines and gets the sense that almost everything to date is recorded above the surface. That most of the clues he wants will be surface-bound. However, most of the surface research appears to be associated with the root area coming somewhere northwest of the Mahruud or the Broken Deep. Almost everything has a relationship to something from there. He is thrown off, however, answers start to appear in a dream state. Every time something appears to be real and not crazy, it was related to a dream state.

Aury, Hap, and Ky read the research that he brings them. We find the info about the arcane waypoint…because we know the one we traveled through was specific to followers of Diverra. These waypoints we know are scattered all over Myrdae and their origin is very, very old - from the Tegenwald Phet in the days before they were banned. The Tegenwald (as they were called a long time ago) historically was good with exploration. And that most of the Bathean Empire was magical back then. There were arcane waypoints that we used widely…then as time went on, restrictions were put in place to limit travel. Notes are found that talk about how some waypoints misfire…where some come and where they go. We find out that some arcane waypoints were made with shortcuts by the Tegenwald…to speed up how they were created. As Hap studies, he finds a journal from a traveler that he finds interesting. It is old and misplaced - shouldn’t be in a shop like this. This is something that should be in the Advents of Harmony - and not here. This is truly misplaced. This traveler documented routes pre-Awakening. He talks about a waypoint he found in the place that is now the lake (this was originally a lowland setting before it was flooded). He notes that there was something different about this particular waypoint (restricted access). Hap then finds a rough sketch of a map. It shows hillsides…path went from lowlands into the hillside. And then shows how the path is blocked - with an entry about how every time he tried he was blocked. He said he saw and smelled roses. So we know we are on the right path. We think the Sanctuary to Diverra is probably somewhere through the Thallgrove…on a path of ascension there somewhere.

Ains asks him if there is anything he can do for favor. He asks if he has any books. Ains has a play that he got from Emberstran theatre that he stole…he shows it to him and says it was from the author. He says that he will give him favor in exchange. Dririel hands Ains an amethyst bead with veins of platinum (Selraeth bead) - giving Ains a bit more favor.

The Soothing Spores

Og goes into the small shop, the Soothing Spores, between the market stalls. He steps in and it smells of warm tea and herbs; crowded shelves, jars with dried fungi. An elderly deep gnome greets him…what can I do for you? Og senses that she is “on” some type of herb. They introduce each other - this is Nelka Nittabomb. He tells her that he was intrigued by the name of her shop. They talk about some energizing snacks, some mood elevating items, some dream-state items. He tells her what drew him there is the fact that he is going to be visiting the Thallgrove. She says that she gets products from there. Og offers to help her with anything to get favor. He asks if she has products that would help with the madness in the Thallgrove. She says that nothing is guaranteed, but has some items that could help. He then asks about prices for some of her items. One is an energizing herb (Glowseed allows you to skip a long rest without exhaustion) \= 5cp. Resistance mushrooms last 8 hours (Valecap Extract - they numb the mind….so it could hurt some spellcasters) \= 12gp per dose. One that is a hallucinogen (Mindbloom) is 10gp. There is another that allows you to explore memories of blocked thoughts \= 15gp. He decides to purchase Valecap, Glowseed x 6, and a Mindbloom.

He then goes through the trading stalls. He sees people with basic stuff to sell - fisher people, craftspeople who carved trinkets from rocks, basic stuff - people trying to make a living. This is a generic day in the market.

Silken Secrets

Ky and Ains visit the Silken Secrets, as Hap and Aury go to visit the Trader’s Tankard. At the shop, Ains walks in. The place is fairly ornate outside, metal door with shiny steel features. Purple/violet color scheme inside. Ains makes the wind blow as he walks in the shop. He had been starting to get a perfume scent…but once we puffs the air it mixes with the smell of incense. Tapestry covered floor…very nice and plush. Tall, perfectly dressed drow looks him over and says nothing. But another drow steps forward to help him. Ains says “finally a place of worth in this town” and is rolling a gem in his finger. He says a place with class, dignity, etc. Ains name drops Endavo. They then have a discussion about how velvet and purple are his colors. He asks the other drow to help him decide what color that Ains is (he mentions the name of the guy that Coppereyes needs information from…and that guy walks up). Then Ky walks in. She asks for a fitting room and she goes inside and changes into her fancy green dress. Meanwhile, Ains discusses colors with him. He asks him about his style…and asks how he normally dresses. He is focusing on him and thinks something is familiar, but Ains cannot place him. He says he is impeccable and not to compare himself. He says he wants to visit Ys’Nonterien and wants to make a good impression and needs his help. He then remembers him from the surface - that someone close to Ains (Tsulanan -maybe this is his uncle or father) was someone he dealt with. Who is Tsulanan to you? This drow says he adventured with Tsulanan. When they were in Ahnassa, fanatics came and took Tsulanan in the night. He then goes to get a journal and brings it to Ains. He feels a ring around his neck burn. This is a journal that I tried to read and cannot. Ains then tries to mage hand the book from him and does. When it is in his hand Ains’ hand is warm. Ky comes out of the fitting room. She asks about an accessory for the dress and the two drow confer and offer something to her to try. While distracted, Ains tries to read the journal, but it is gibberish. He feels like this is the biggest find he has come across. They tell Ky that the scarf is 8 silver pieces and that she looks very nice. She thinks it is strange that they are complimenting her but thinks this is a fair price. Ains then tells the drow that the man is his grandfather, but that he hasn’t revealed that to many people because the fanatical cult group is after him. He has been looking for pieces of information about his grandfather. Ky picks up on this conversation, pays them the 8 sp for the scarf, and intimidates the drow into giving him the book. The drow just asks that if Ains gets answers that he will come back and tell them. Ky comes back out of the dressing room, picks up her scarf and is now wearing her fine blue cloak. Ains tells him he is Anaron. The drow tells her about the fine spider silk that the scarf is made of - will offer special properties above ground when she’s on the surface (rain won’t penetrate, will keep her cool). He says this is the finest silk YOU will ever have. And she says, “Have you ever been to Dunduar?” As he almost spits and says no, she says, “I didn’t think so…you have a nice day” and leaves.

The Trader’s Tankard

Meanwhile, at the pub, which overlooks the Rothenloch (both peaceful and eerie), there is a hum of conversation. There is a bar in the middle and booths around the sides. They see Durak members here. “What can I get you?” Beer. Beer it is. Would you rather have a mug each or a gallon? They each get a mug and they look over and they see She’nog in here having a drink. Well - I didn’t think I’d see you here, but here you are. Want to join us? Sure - if you are in the mood for a sad tale. She says her fishing hole is not well. She says she can’t get in because of Stone Giants. They are just throwing rocks into the water…so she can’t get in. They aren’t even fishing. Aury offers to take care of the problem. She says she and her sisters at the Fishery & Smokehouse would be very pleased. But she says “do you want to be crushed by boulders?” She says she saw 2 of them…they are giants. If you think you can handle them, I’ll take you there. Hap asks how big are they again? And they laugh. Aury suggests that maybe they could be talked into moving to another fishing hole. She says she knows of another one or two places that maybe we could move them to - especially one that is one of her competitors' places. They say that they will get their other members to come and help.

The rest of the group meets Hap and Aury at the tavern and learns about the giant problem. We spend the day in the town and hang out and then go back to the Draught to spend the night.

We think Thallgrove is where we need to go to find a path of ascension up there to find the Sanctuary.

We know we could commission a boat to take us or ask She’nog to take us - and perhaps help her along the way.

NEXT TIME - we will start the next day and hear about Ains book study and make a decision about where we are going.

– The End of the Session –

Giant’s Silver

In-World26th of Talil, 1246 WhereNunglthil PlayedJanuary 28, 2026

25th of Talil in the Darkbottom Draught

We all meet back at the Draught and go to our rooms (Ains, Og, and Ky’tha in one room; Hap and Aury in one; Zymve has her own room that she gets at a discount because of her 6-bead favor she’s earned). Ains is interested in the journal he got and starts to flip through it, but he can’t read it (strange, angular script). But he studies it and stares at it and something starts to become clear: this isn’t a language that is meant to be translated. He starts to feel pressure behind his eyes and gets a mild headache…and Og and Ky notice that he is getting a soft glow around his neck (from his family necklace). The script starts to reveal words now - and he starts to read the title which says “Waiting Beneath the Name.” He can’t make out words underneath…so he flips pages and the journal starts to feel heavy, weighing him down. He then makes out another section “When Waking Fails.” Og and Ky notice he looks very uncomfortable. He then flips more and it says “The Key Ring.” He is really hurting now. The glow on the necklace fades and he can’t read anything…so he closes the journal and falls asleep promptly.

A dream comes back to Ains - it is of he and Og in Emberstran running. As Ains sleeps, Og suggests that he and Ky pick up the book. Og can’t make anything out so he passes it to Ky and she thinks maybe it can be decoded, but nothing is obvious. They both go to bed.

26th of Talil

We all wake up the next day and go down to the dining room to eat breakfast. The same person (Tikrin) asks “the usual” and we say yes - but each picks the type of meal. We talk about Aury’s finer tastes - especially since he has given up so much (i.e., women - “the only woman in my life is my goddess.”). Ky comments on Zymve’s appearance and asks if she had a big day yesterday. She replies that it is too early for questions.

The group discusses what we’d like to do today and talk about taking care of the stone giant problem for She’nog’s sister or going ahead to hire a boat to take the group to Thallgrove. We discuss the warnings about Thallgrove and talk about possible ways to mitigate some of the danger (potion Og purchased, covering our noses and mouths). Zymve tells the group that the spores in Thallgrove would enter via the skin. More discussion about giants or Thallgrove. Aury points out that we might not be able to come back to this area once we get to the sanctuary of Diverra - assuming we find it.

We decide to help She’nog’s sister with the stone giant problem. Hap, Aury, Ky, and Og go to find She’nog. Zymve goes to the Soothing Spores and Ains goes to see Copper Eyes.

Gemcraft Store

Ains enters the store. He talks to Copper Eyes about what he should have made for the drow. He finds out that he had made the previous item out of gold and a ruby. Ains suggests that he uses platinum and amethyst/purple gems. He is happy with the suggestion and says that if it works, he will give Ains some favor. Ains also asks if he has another 300gp diamond.

Soothing Spores Shop

Zymve steps into the Soothing Spores and talks to Nelka. She asks her about fungi - specifically about Thallgrove. She tells her that she learned everything she knows from a little guy who is not often around - his type is resistant to the fungi there. He told her that very few people go in and come back out…that people go mad when they get there. How do you protect yourself? Don’t go in. I have some extracts that might help - Valecap extracts (10 potions). I can sell them to you for 9gp/each. I will sell you 2 for 17gp. She agrees and purchases the extract.

Fishery & Smokehouse

The rest of the group makes their way to the docks near the Fishery & Smokehouse. We don’t see She’nog, but are standing in front of the establishment and smell the aroma of fish. We decide to go in and find She’nog’s sister. This is a 2-story place - the lower floor has fresh fish and upstairs is a loft area with preservation racks. We see a fair-skinned, dark auburn hair half-orc female and another up top who is similar looking (maybe twins), both who favor She’nog. We greet one of them and tell why we are there. Soon after, She’nog arrives. She is disappointed in her catch of the day so far…the quantity of the fish is lower than usual. We tell her that we will help her and ask what we could get in exchange - and they answer that the Kar’solar would be happy with our help (would give us favor). Ky asks if there are some in that group that would be able to sail us to Thrallgrove. She says she knows of a couple of ships, but maybe the only one big enough is Valor’s Steelwake…or the duergar Grendal might have the best one to take us. About that time, Ains and Zymve come in and we talk about going today.

Giant Contraption

We make our way out to She’nog’s boat and we board, after she makes is stable and safe for travel. Og volunteers to help paddle. Hap says he will, too. We make our way across Rothenloch - not the way we came initially, but to her fishing hole. Hap says he would like to send Fleur up as we start to get close. We travel about an hour and she says the hole is around the bend and Hap asks her to stop to allow Fleur to fly up. As soon as Fleur goes around the bend, she sees that the fishing hole is right there. She’nog, meanwhile, ties off to the side of the lake where there is a slight path (not cliffs). Fleur sees a wide ring of massive boulders and a giant standing there and reach into the hole and pull up a bucket and then turn and dump it out. And she sees a massive pickaxe where it looks like the giant has been working. It looks like he’s created a well - but a dry well. Hap tells the group what Fleur saw. The well is 40-50’ off shore in the water, and is about 25’ wide and deep enough for another giant to be standing inside, handing the bucket up to the giant on top. We discuss what the giants might be doing.

Ains and Og decide to walk over on the path, conversing in giant “what a nice night.” They hear one of the giants yell “we have company”. Ky’tha (15) and Zymve (invisible) stealth behind them; with Hap and Aury coming afterwards (by 90’). “You are not welcome here” they yell and they tell Ains and Og to leave. Ains and Og try to play the “brotherhood” card, but it doesn’t seem to work well. The giant tells them they do not belong here. “Why” asks Ains. They say they have taken claim of this area. Ains says they are reps of the “Boulder Throwers” of the DIstancion Mountains and they are here to offer them aid, comfort, and money. “How much money” the giant asks. The giant says to come back in 3 days so I can bargain with you. Og asks them what they are doing and the giant says that they should know if they are brothers. Ains says they deal with matters of the sky and not water. Ains tries to persuade them to tell him what they are doing. The giant says he will tell them what they are doing if Ains and Og will leave. Og says they will come back in 3 days. The giant says they are mining here and it has taken them days to build this. Ains asks what they are mining for. They say “the stuff in the hole” and come back in 3 days and we won’t be here and you can do whatever you want. Ains turns around and “pretends” like he is talking to the Chieftain who is around the corner. The giants don’t buy this.

IT IS INITIATIVE - Combat vs. the Stone Giants
  • ROUND ONE: Aury moves up to Og, Ains, and Ky’tha and casts bless on each of them. Zymve casts darkness on one of the giants. We hear the giant moving rocks and he steps outside of the darkness and tosses a rock to Ains and Aury casts reaction of warding flare to make the giant disadvantaged and Ains shields himself and the giant misses and steps back into the darkness. Ains touches Og and casts a spell and hastes him and then steps back further. The other giant climbs out of the well and sets down the big bucket. Og runs into the darkness and blindly swings at the giant and hits twice. Hap runs forward and has Fleur fly to the second giant we can see and delivers a chill touch spell and misses. Ky’tha dashes forward and then takes a bonus action to hide.
  • ROUND TWO: Aury moves up next to Hap and waits to see if there is a need for a reaction. Zymve casts a spell on the giant she can see - dissonant whispers - and he takes damage and is forced to move to the other side of the well’s ring and is frightened of Zymve. The first giant moves out of the darkness and swings his axe at Ky’tha. He misses the first swing, but hits with the second attack and she dodges half of the damage. Ains casts a spell on the giant using Aury to help aim and misses. The second giant reaches down and grabs two boulders and throws them to Hap and Aury. He misses Aury, fumbling. And he misses Hap, too, and falls prone. Og moves up and attacks the giant on the ground and misses (but grazes) and then attacks again and hits twice. Hap has Fleur deliver a chill touch on the prone giant. Ky’tha steadies herself and has advantage and hits with her dagger…but has to stay put.
  • ROUND THREE: Aury stays put and casts guiding bolt on the giant next to Ky’tha and misses. Zymve casts a cloud of daggers on the giant nearest her. That giant then moves out of the cloud and attacks Ky’tha again, missing first, then hitting her - and she again, dodges some of the damage. He then moves near Zymve. Ains asks Aury for help and shoots a spell at the prone giant with disadvantage and hits. That giant then stands up and tries to grab Og and does and then drops Og into the pit 50’ down, damaging him. At the bottom, he sees the biggest silver ore chunk he has ever seen. That giant moves around the ring of the well. Og tries to climb out of the well and gets almost to the top, but can’t get all the way out, as he slips a little. He then uses an action to try to get up and slides a little further down and is now 10’ from the top of the well. Hap moves up to the giant close by and hits with his new weapon and hits, then tries to hit with his dagger and hits. He then attacks again with the new weapon and hits again. Then attacks with the dagger again and hits and kills it. Ky’tha steadies herself and shoots her hand crossbow and the remaining giant and hits.
  • ROUND FOUR: Aury moves a little and stays close to Ains and casts light on himself so Ains can see close up. Zymve moves up closer to the remaining giant and casts dancing lights on the giant to light him up and inspires Og. Ains thinks the giant is bloodied and glowing purple energy flows around him and he lifts off the ground and he shoots a lightning bolt at the giant, who saves, but still takes damage. The giant looks at Og and says “representatives my ass,” and dives into the water and starts swimming away. Og climbs out of the well and jumps in the water to swim after him. He attacks and misses all three efforts. Hap tells Fleur to fly to him and he tries to chill touch and misses. Ky’tha dashes forward to the end of the well, shouting to She’nog to take her boat out.
  • ROUND FIVE: Aury cast a fireball into the water, trying to hit the giant. He does, but the damage is lessened because of the water. Zymve dashes forward 60’. Ains steps to the shore and shoots a sorcerers’ burst and hits the giant. The giant turns around and swims to Og and tries to grab him and gets him and swims down with him, taking his loin cloth off. Og attacks underwater and hits with 3 attacks, hitting critically on the last hit, then hits with a fourth attack, killing him. The group sees water pool up and then Og pops up and swims to shore.

Ky’tha and Zymve peer down into the hole, as Zymve moves her dancing lights into the well and they see a mound of silver below the surface. Og says he saw one chunk of silver down there. She’nog moves her boat around the bend and sees the dead giants and the well. Ky’tha takes two ropes and ties them together and affixes it to the bucket and ties it off to the boat. We lower Hap down on the bucket as Og climbs down. Once they are down, Ky’tha goes down. Ky looks at the ore and can’t make out the total size and Hap tries to swim under the water to figure it out, too. Both aren’t sure how big it is. They try to lower Ains into the hole and he takes damage, hitting his head as their grip slips.

Ains tries to shatter some of the rock around the ore with his spell. He thinks it will be 100 pounds or more of silver. He is able to loosen the ore from the rock. We decide to get the people up out of the hole with ropes and then tie off the ore to have Og and Hap pull it up. We get it to the top and Og carries it on to the boat. We tie the bucket on to the boat and one of us travels in the bucket (Ky’tha). Travel back to the town is slower, but we arrive back at the dock.

She’nog speaks on our behalf to her sisters upon our arrival and we are each issued favor of Kar’Solar. The group decides to take the ore to sell to the duergars.

Firmforge

The group goes to the forge - Firmforge - to try to sell the ore. As they walk in, there is a large forge and three anvils. There are two gray skinned dwarves and we talk to them about it. They agree that this is 200 pounds. We tell them that we think it is worth 1000gp, and they counter with 800gp. Ains counters with 850 plus favors for each, but Ky’tha is not convinced. Zymve flaunts her favor and convinces them to give us 900gp, plus favor.

A Ride on Grindle’s Chain

After the sale, we go to try to find a captain to agree to take us the next day. We see a cargo ship that looks captained by a duergar, that looks like it could take all of us. Aury asks about the extract he asked Zymve to buy and she gives it to him (says she bought one for 12gp). We approach the captain, Grindle Ashwake. He initially tells us 36gp for the lot of us for 3 days, but Ky’tha mentions that we just took down a couple of stone giants, and Zymve intimidates him so we only have to pay 30gp. He says to provision up and we will leave soon.

Message from Lady Rook

We all grab our supplies from the Draught and Og goes to buy fishing supplies. We meet back at the docks and board the ship. We pull out of the port. Ky’tha hears a voice in her head “The Council has decided and Lady Rook is appreciative. She has concerns about your locations with the marrowstone and she could use your help back in Emberstran.” As we travel, we hear fishermen yelling at each other over the water - arguing about their fishing hole. Ains uses a sending spell to put words into one’s head to make them fight each other - stirring up trouble. We get a rest that night

27th of Talil - next morning

As we continue and are waking up, Zymve feels like the ceiling of the cavern is crashing and opening…and it does. As the rocks shatter into the water, tentacles reach up out of the water and grab her. She doesn’t see any of her friends. Suddenly the vision ends and he gasps and is out of breath, waking up. She thinks she was possessed by the Shadow of Rothenloch. We all see that she is uncomfortable and gasping. We discuss what happened to her. We think it was a dream, but she thinks it was real.

28th of Talil - next morning

We see that Grindle is avoiding someone as we travel. He points and Zymve and Ky’tha see smugglers in the distance, but he navigates us around them without incident. We finally pass them.

29th of Talil - next day

We end up making it to a shore and see a massive cave mouth opening up ahead. There are no torches lit, but we see light ahead in the cave. We smell rich soil, sweet bark…we think we have made it to Thallgrove. We part ways with the Captain.

Cave to enter Thallgrove

We make our way to the cave and colors begin to emerge. The air is cooler. We see fungi of various colors. Blues, rust colors…some are small, but others are the size of trees. We notice tiny motes and spores that are filling the air. Ky’tha and everyone wrap scarves around their faces, only leaving their eyes exposed. Time to make wisdom saving throws. Ains has tourette’s syndrome telepathically and starts saying things in other party member’s heads. Aury is fine. Hap swears that the fungi is talking to him. Og also thinks the fungi are talking to him. Zymve is okay for now. Ky’tha starts getting very emotional and crying for no reason. Her tears almost glows. The air is starting to make us a little feverish, like our skin is crawling.

Dead People

Up ahead we see a boot print that fungus has attached to and there is a coil of rope on ground. There are uneven steps. And then a body with a breastplate and sword sitting next to them. Eyes stare ahead. We see fungus growing up around the body. We see another person up ahead in robes lying face down, grasping a tome, with fungus growing up around them. Ains pulls the tome to him - it is a spellbook. He casts detect magic and sees none other than the tome. Aury examines the bodies to see if he can tell what is going on. He thinks lesser restoration might be the only thing that could help.

Makra the Genling

We travel further. Ky’tha notices movement ahead. She sees a small creature ahead…small and wiry. Unblinking eyes. She asks - are you a [genling](https://www.dndbeyond.com/monsters/6091146-genling-minion)? A thought presses into Og’s mind: “Wow - you are big.” Og replies out loud “yes, I am big.” Zymve hears “you understand, don’t you?” And she says in her head “yes, I do.” But they do not understand her either. Ky’tha tries to touch her and tries to talk to her, but she doesn’t understand. Same with Ains. Finally, Zymve speaks in deep speech and they can talk. “Why are you here?” they ask in deep speech telepathically. Zymve responds that we are looking for a temple. He says Makra…introducing himself. He says he will take us home to his village. Ky’tha asks Zymve to ask if they are genlings. She does and he responds that they are. Zymve agrees that we will go to his village. Aury casts Lesser Restoration on Ky’tha to make her stop crying and it works. Zymve asks if he knows how we can avoid the effects of the land. He doesn’t, but thinks maybe someone in his village will know.

The session ends as we are walking to Makra’s genling village.

(Each character has gained enough XP to make it to level 7)

– The End of the Session –

In Bimblefol

In-World29th of Talil, 1246 WhereThallgrove PlayedFebruary 17, 2026

Escort to Genling Village

Walking in the cavern with violet and amber glow from fungi, we see spores in the air (land like snowflakes). We are following Makra. Ky’tha is no longer crying. Makra guides the group through various tunnels. He speaks telepathically to Zymve “the ground listens” telling the group to be quiet here and there (holding his finger up to his trunk). We move around and the fungi shift colors - sometimes more blue, others orange, yellow, etc. We continue and pause here and there…then pick the pace back up.

Whenever Makra sees/hears anyone in the group talk he says to Zymve “Bimblefol” and tilts his head to the side. Then continues. Every now and then Zymve notices that Makra is hitting a piece of fungus with his staff (Og and Hap hear “ouch”). He throws those pieces in a sack. He is collecting while we walk, but no one really notices this.

We enter another area and the fungi is very thick here. We see him “sniffing” the air. He darts over to a wall…and barely visible he reaches and grabs and tiny violet pod and he sniffs it and it pulses and he runs over to Og and gives it to him. Og tries to listen and thinks it is praying. Makra tells Zymve that Og should eat it. Og eats it. Hap hears it say “oh gross” as Og chews it. Og is then cured of his condition. Makra says that is a rare fungi, but he will look for it. Zymve asks if it cures everything and he says it does.

We continue on. As Ains continues to twitch and yell things, Aury cures him (lesser restoration). We continue forward.

Thall Mound Chamber

There is a waterfall in this new cavern and it is orange colored. The far back wall suddenly looks like it shifts. We all hear Makra make an audible noise and he hides. We see an outline of a shadowy creature hold something up and start to eat it (it is one of Makra’s buddies).

IT IS INITIATIVE: Combat vs. Thall Mounds
  • ROUND ONE: Ains casts haste on Og and shoves him forward 5’. Zymve casts a spell on the creature and it failed its save, taking psychic damage and disadvantage on his next attack then runs to hide behind a close fungi. Makra hid by the back wall with his staff at the ready. Og flies into a rage and moves forward and attacks him three times, hitting all times. He then tries to restrain him with vines, but the giant succeeds. Spores fall off of the creature onto Og and he fails his CON save and is stunned. He then reaches down and hits Og and is grappled and has the poisoned condition. He then attacks again and fumbles, falls, then stands back up. And takes Og and moves forward. Aury casts a spell on him and the creature fails his save and takes radiant damage. Aury then moves near Zymve. Another creature suddenly emerges from another mound near Zymve and Aury and attacks Hap with spores, stunning him. He then moves next to Zymve and grabs her, giving her temp hit points, as she takes damage, as she is grappled and has the poisoned condition. He then moves forward to attack Aury with Zymve in hand and misses him. Ky’tha moves up to hide from the one holding Og and tries to shoot the new one, but misses. Hap is stunned, but frees himself at the end of his turn.
  • ROUND TWO: Ains casts a spell to make himself purple and then moves forward a little and casts a sorcerer’s burst at the one on Og and hits with thunder damage, then moves back. Zymve casts mirror image on herself and then tries to charm the creature. Makra tries to use his blow gun and misses. Og is stunned and continues to be stunned. The creature with Og tries to shove him down his throat and does (he is still grappled, stunned, poisoned, restrained, and would take acid damage (but he’s resistant). He then moves forward and tries to stun Ky’tha with spores. He then burrows 10’ into the ground. Aury casts a spell on the one nearest him and hits as the creature takes radiant damage. That creature then releases spores onto Aury, as he sets Zymve down. Aury is stunned and he tries to grab him and does - he is grappled, poisoned, and takes damage. The creature then moves forward to attack Hap and fumbles, allowing Hap an attack of opportunity, but he misses. Hap tries to run away toward the hole where the creature burrowed and the other creature misses with his attack of opportunity. Hap makes it to the hole and attacks that creature and then frightens him. He then misses with his second attack. He tries a third time and hits. Ky’tha moves to the hole and attacks the creature. Ky hits the creature that is holding Aury with a sneak attack and doesn’t move.
  • ROUND THREE: Ains moves away from creature two and tries to pull Aury from the creature, and he is released from the grapple. He then sorcerer’s burst that creature and inflicts thunder damage. Zymve dashes to the hole and gives an inspiration to Hap. Makra crits on the creature near Aury with his blow gun, making him have disadvantage on his next attack. Og breaks from the stun, but he’s still in the hole and being compressed. He is no longer poisoned. He has two points of exhaustion. The creature rolls around and grabs Hap, grappling him, poisoning him, and doing some damage. He takes damage from a hellish rebuke (just 2 points), but it kills him anyway. Hap is no longer grappled. Aury is stunned and breaks it at the end of his turn. He is no longer grappled, poisoned, or stunned. The creature then tries to grab Aury again and Aury fires off a flare and the creature fumbles. He tries to hit Aury again and does - he is grappled and stunned. He then moves toward Ky’tha and is no longer charmed. Hap runs over to the other creature and misses with his first attack, then hits. He then hits two more times. He is frightened of Hap now and takes damage. Ky’tha disengages and moves back, but gets to shoot her bow with advantage from vex (last turn) and hits with a sneak attack, then hides behind a rock outcropping.
  • ROUND FOUR: Ains hits with a sorcerer’s burst and does more damage to the creature - then moves to the hole and uses telekinetic shove to impose advantage for Og to try to get out. Zymve uses mockery on the creature, who fails, and he takes psychic damage and is now disadvantaged. Makra uses mind sliver to impose psychic damage on the creature. Og tries to push the dead creature off him and crits - pushing the guy out of the hole with ease. He then crawls out of the hole, muddy and smelly, and moves to the other creature and hits twice, killing it.

Makra runs over to Zymve and says “wow” and then sees the dead creatures in the hole - 2 of his friends and says “Bimblefol” and points. He is asking us to take the two with us. Og runs over to grab them and picks them up.

Ains keeps his telepathic bond with Og.

Bimblefol

We make our way to Bimblefol. There are huge plant structures rising from a lake in a huge cavern with rope bridges stretched between them. The structure in the center is a little different - more fungal covered. Makra takes us to a small hut at the edge of the lake. Another Makra is there - a female. She checks us out and then nods at Makra and grabs a small decanter off of a shelf (top is glowing). She pours tiny cups of this liquid and hands them to each of us. We smell earthy sweetness and we all take the shot (except Ains who waits to see what happens). We feel a slight headache and then we can now hear them. She says “you can hear us” to each one of us. Ky’tha says “can you hear us.” and she says “I’m Betuu.” Makra tells her that we didn’t all fall to glow. And she says “not yet.” She says you may walk the bridges. Ky’tha asks Betuu about the violet fungi. Betuu asks Makra if he found any. He says he only found one and Batuu says “maybe Tatti has it.” Og says “I’m very sorry about this, but where shall I put them (referring to the dead bodies he carries)?” And she says “I’ll get someone - let them rest here.”

Aury asks if there is a sanctuary around here. Betuu says that we should go to the elder (Aniba) if we want to find information (and get distracted with images in the mind and touch and taste and smell) Makra says he will take us. Betuu points to various mushrooms and tells us who is where. As we start to go, we head to the Watch tower (we see several gemlings with blowguns). Zymve goes first across the first bridge. It holds her fine and she crosses by herself. Ains steps on it and crosses - no issues. Aury then goes across after Ains has finished his trek across. We hear gemlings talk about how big we all are. After he finishes, Ky’tha starts to cross as it sags and creaks. After she finishes, Hap crosses, and it creaks again and sags, but he makes it. Og decides to stay at the hut, but Makra insists that he comes along. He grabs the chest to start dragging it across. Og starts to try to cross and the bridge gets really close to the water in the center, but he makes it across to the Watch Tower. We hear other gemlings badmouth Makra for bringing the group to their village. They say “well, we are standing watch.” They are looking for threatening creatures. There are big creatures that float and have a bunch of eyeballs and others that fly and have leather wings. We get the feeling that they are upset with us for disrupting their watch. Aury tries to convince them that since we are not a danger don’t focus on us.

Bimblefol Sanctum

We then proceed to the bridge to the Sanctum. This was a large thick, hollowed out rock. We step inside and it is cool with petrified mushroom that looks and feels like stone. There is a big open area. There is a big, plump fat gemling in a huge, oversized chair at the far side. Behind her we see a shelf with strange objects - bones, metal…we aren’t sure. These don’t look like Underdark items - she is a collector of other items. “Ahhhh-large walkers. Upright thunder shapes. Ummm..Makra…good collectibles, I approve.” She speaks “stronger.” You stand before Aniba and we see a symbol on the ground - Zymve looks at it. I have survived, I say seated. All important matters pass through me. She looks at Og - you are damp with service and it is fascinating. Zymve makes out “wisdom’s final form” on the ground. How can Aniba assist you? The group looks at Aury. “I have been sent by my goddess to find her Sanctuary.” I have been told it may be in this area. “Bimblefol has no such sanctuary, for I am the wisdom in its final form.” Ains speaks up - “my acquaintance only seeks guidance here because no other place has such reverence here as this place. Truly his goddess sent us to you.” Who might be his goddess? The goddess of roses and love. Ahhhh - Diverra. My friend here would like to know if there is any chance that your servants have looked around the area and might have found something - a place touched by Diverra. She says she might know of a place because she is wise. She pats the side of her big chair - the forgotten surface grove - I know what you speak of. Meanwhile, Zymve sees a dog skull, a pressed flower, a horseshoe. Ains asks if she will grace us with directions to this place. She says that she can’t take us. Ains asks if Makra can take us and she says that she has a map. She says maps are valuable, what do you have to offer. Aury offers a tinderbox, Ky’tha offers a fancy blue cloak, Og offers a bottle of wine. She seems interested in the wine and offers to swap the map for it. She hands the map to Og…and also the dog skull because she thinks the wine was too much. She loves it and samples it.

We examine the map. It is a fungal parchment that looks delicate. Og can almost see through it. She says it is special and take care of it. We see Bimblefol on the map and as Og turns it, he thinks he sees the part she is talking about. She has lots of maps under her chairs. On the map there is an X…and lots of big wavy lines on it (we think it might be water). She says “oh - this might be a problem - none of you breathe water.” Ains thinks she is very confident in what she is saying. She asks can you bend the weave to your favor? If not, head to Tatti. Anything else for the wise, she asks. Zymve asks if they have music. Not really. Ains asks if they have arcane components (things to bend the weave). She asks where we are from - we say the surface, but came via Nunglthil. She says, “you should visit the wisdom before you leave.” Aury doesn’t think this is an arcane waypoint, but he feels like there is a sense of the weave here in this room. Aury asks if there is anything she wants. She says - will you visit me from the surface. He says “I don’t see why not.” She longs for trinkets from the surface - things that don’t belong in the Underdark. You are friends of the wisdom and I shall call you “friends of the wisdom.” You should feel blessed. Aury casts comprehend languages to read the inscription on the floor.

Bimblefol Soup Kitchen

Og and Zymve take off to the next mushroom to find the soup. Makra is yelling “there’s rules, there’s rules.” But the two are ahead of everyone else. As they walk into the Soup Kitchen, they see a long stone counter with large kettles that have deep speech above them. There are several gemlings in line. One cuts in line and there is a loud bang from another (Kasem) “line has order and order has line” and “no broth for you.” They are in line.

The rest of the group goes to the Soup Kitchen. Zymve gets the Rootcoil Steep; Og gets Stoneflower Bisque; Ky’tha steps out to pour out her waterskin and then gets the violet soup in her skin to take with her (it will remove minor spore symptoms); Ains takes the Stoneflower Bisque; Aury takes Violet Pod Mash and pours it in Ky’tha’s waterskin; Hap takes the Violet Pod mash and eats it immediately. Makra gives some ingredients to Kasem.

Bimblefol Commons

As the group crosses over the bridge to the Commons, Aury and Ky’tha eat a ration. Bridges dip; we see gemlings making bets. We make it over and see that everyone is just hanging out. This is an open area with lofts - people who have nowhere else to be, hang out here. Zymve decides to try to play her instrument for the group. She shares an original song and everyone looks up to watch and hear her. She plays very well. Some like this and start trying to pick up a beat, as they watch Ains try to dance. Some come down from the lofts and gather around. They ask who we are and what is happening. This seems special since it is noise they aren’t used to. Some get disturbing close, but she keeps them at bay. We think we could rest here if needed. She says “You are the most cultured people I have ever encountered.” They are excited and clap for her and she feels amazing. Zymve says she is going to stay here while we go find Tatti. Ains creates a bond with her.

Bimblefol Alchemist

We then leave to go find Tatti, the genling alchemist. We get to the Chief mushroom and there are two ways to go. We see a door that is closed (upper level) and then head over to the Mixologist. Ains asks Makra about his opinion of the Chief and he says “I like Aniba.” Ky’tha asks what he is doing behind the closed door and Makra replies that he is sleeping.

The group goes to the next mushroom. It is very bright inside. There are many glowing bulbs hanging inside. It is the closest we have been to the surface since we arrived in the Underdark. Herbal smell, not neat. Many bottles - stuff everywhere. Boxes overflowing with fungi. Bundles of roots. Various vials. A bald gemling standing in the center wrapped in strips of fungus that are so layered that it seems like a robe (browns, rusts, grays). She is pouring liquids. She says in our heads “do not bump into that table.” She says “oh - large ones” and pauses. You carry sunlight things. She says we smell of sky. We ask if she has ever been to the surface and she has not. Aury looks around to read what she has.

Ky’tha trades her candle for a Stillbreath Filter (waterbreathing for 1 hour). Aury offers a Censer of Levitation for 3 potions of Stillbreath Filter. Og offers some leaves in exchange for a Stillbreath Filter. And offers the dog skull for a Violet Quiet Pod elixir.

Hap pulls out a block of incense and lights it up. He says you can have this for a Stillbreath Filter. She says she needs more and Ky’tha intimidates her, asking Hap to light it again. And she allows the trade.

We head back to the Commons to get a rest. We get a long rest.

30th of Talil - Leaving Bimblefol

The next day, we leave the village and follow the path on the map that we believe will lead us to the Lost Sanctuary of Diverra. The path leads through a shallow channel. Water is clear looking, but we think it isn’t as clear as it looks (can’t see our feet, but it looks clear). We make our way through the tunnels lined with fungi and water. We have to crouch sometimes, tunnels vary by size. We get several hours away and the stream deepens into a tunnel that tightens and we see a path up ahead that we are going to have to climb.

Ky’tha climbs up and ties her rope off to something and throws the other end down. Aury makes his way up. They are on a natural shelf (dry air). Hap goes next - no issues (20). Zymve goes next and she makes it up easily. Og then goes up without issue. Ains then ties off to the rope and pulls him up without issue. All are now on the shelf.

We press on through another tunnel. It is quiet. We start to feel that we are getting further from the fungi, from Thallgrove.

Rest for the Night – Ky’tha’s Hallucination

After another 3-4 hours, we find an opening where we can set up camp for the night. We set up watch. Aury and Ky’tha take first watch. Aury thinks about getting to the sanctuary - he looks over at Ky’tha and she is zoning out, too. Ky’tha is zoning out and gets lost in her thoughts, thinking am I here, not here as the stone dissolves around her. A memory awakens, as if the time in the Thallgrove awakened something tucked away forever. She is in the middle of Dunduar’s ancestral hall, lined with precious metals. She feels a call/pull as the metals focus her on the symbol of Muerg in the halls of Dunduar (she had never noticed this before). Her family stands before her - “we cannot leave it open. It will be sealed forever.” Then she hears “why” (from someone she doesn’t know - they must be Cragfury). She sees her grandmother. That Cragfury says “the flow must remain.” Aury thinks Ky’tha is daydreaming, and doesn't see danger. Aury thinks Ky’tha is excited about something. Ky’tha sees Graddock slam his fist “flow destroys, we bind it, we seal it…and laughs…we prosper.” Grandmother looks at her. Then the memory shifts–she’s at the bottom of a mine shaft. Further down the mineshaft is a beautifully carved circular stone ring around the tunnel, the stone accented by metal threads, like veins through the stone–enlightened with the weave. She sees a recession on the side of the stone and her locket glows. Ky’tha’s heart beats faster. Aury thinks something is up - is concerned. Ky’tha hears “seal it forever” and the other guy says “nothing is forever beneath the stone” and the mine collapses into water (something very out of place and more of a vision than a memory at this time). The shocking change pulled her back to reality. Aury and Ky discuss what happened…was this the past or a foretelling? Was this the effects of fungi? Who knows? Ky’tha feels like she needs to go home…to make sure all is well.

– The End of the Session –
The Chronicle of Emberstran · Chapter 19
