Lost Sanctuary

In-World30th of Talil, 1246 WhereUnderdark PlayedMarch 2, 2026

Ky’tha and Aury wake up Og and Ains. We brief them on the fact that the fungi might cause hallucinations, but otherwise, there was no danger. Aury and Ky’tha get rest, while the other two are at watch. They talk about the water and what to expect, specifically about octopus/octopi…and do we talk to them or fight them, should we encounter them in the water. Watch is uneventful and they wake up Zymve and Hap and get rest. They briefly talk - Hap says “it is pret’ner feels like home” as they talk about the swamp and gators - he describes what they are to Zymve. Night passes uneventfully.

The Crimson Lake — 31st of Talil, 1246

We gather everything up and head out. Ky’tha and Zymve lead - about 20’ ahead of Og and Hap; followed by Ains and Aury. After a couple of hours, we see a dim, constant red light ahead and the cavern opens up. There is a massive underground lake in front of us. The red light is from a crimson-colored fungus from the ceiling. It looks like veins/strands going through the rock, and gives off a red glow. The surface of the water is blood red. As we look, we get the taste and smell of salt water…we remember the initial arcane waypoint when we were first under the sea in that bubble. Aury also picks up a hint of rose in the air. This is mentioned. Zymve asks what a rose is. We see other caves/tunnels across the lake (would take a 10-15 minute swim across). We talk about how to cross and decide to call Fleur (Hap’s familiar owl) out.

Fleur is going to do a low flyover to check to see if anything is moving below the surface. She sees a few shadows in the water, fish, etc. She notices that the saltwater is being fed from an area. Og studies the map and realizes that the “X” is in the middle of this lake. We think we are going to have to drink the potions, but Og says he will scout first. He jumps in to swim and look around. He gets to the center of the lake and goes under the surface to look. He sees something 50’ down, but isn’t sure what it is. Looks like a stone structure, but not a building. He communicates this telepathically to Ains. He dives down to check and sees this is an stone ring that is about 8 feet in diameter sitting on the bottom of the lake, standing upright. This is exactly what Ky’tha saw in her vision last night. She shares this with the group and they all decide to drink their potions and go under water. Aury and Hap walk in, while Ky’tha and Zymve swim down. Ains waits for Og to surface and they both drink their potions and then both swim down.

The Gate

As the group descends, they can look up to see the red surface of the water. We gather around the stone circle/ring that is mounted there; this is exactly with Ky’tha saw. It is metallic with gold threads. Aury thinks this must be the gate he heard about related to the lost gate of the sanctuary. He knows it was only created by or for a god or demi-god. Aury starts to smell roses again. He thinks he is at the right place. He kneels in front of the gate and uses channel divinity to ask for guidance. He puts his hands on the stone “is this where you need us to be?” We see the metallic veins the rock glow and the water “trembles” we see radiant light start to fill in the circle/ring. Looks like surface within the water start to change into a portal. He stands up and walks through with the group following. As Ky’tha walks through she thinks that the ring she saw in her vision was likely a Muerg ring - almost like this one, but a little different.

The Lost Sanctuary

We all walk through and step into an area on the surface. The gate now stands behind us. There is a clearing with vines and wildflowers climbing over everything. There are trees that create a hidden grove, there are roses everywhere, the light is muted…we would have expected it to be brighter. Something is amiss. There is a stone temple. Aury smells roses, but all else smell earth and decay. There is a large tree that is dying with peeling bark, the grass around it is barren. The temple seems very old - maybe an original inspiration to Diverra’s rise to power in the Awakening. Aury is pulled toward the temple, while the rest of the group senses something is up. Og is pulled to the tree. His thornleaf is warm and itchy on his arm…he doesn’t know what to make of us, but he almost gets a feeling like he belongs - like he is supposed to be here.

Ains dries everyone off with prestidigitation. Hap looks around for threats as Ains feels like this is similar to the grove for Diverra that we were in before. We all start to walk forward. Dark shapes start to burst out of the earth…humanoid, then into treelike shapes. The air fills with “defilers turn back.”

IT IS INITIATIVE: Combat vs. the Blight
  • Round One: We see creatures with black wispy-ness around them. They are tree-like. Vines (like roots) spring up around Zemve, Og, Hap, and Ains to grab them. And branches pop off trees and land on the ground and are needle blights in front of the group. Blight tries to Ains with needles and misses. Another blight fumbles attacking Og. The last one attacks Aury and misses. The vine on Ains grapples him, inflicting damage. The one on Og grapples him and inflicts damage. Zymve is also grappled and takes damage. Hap shrugs off his attack. Og rages and swings at the vine on Ains and misses. He then swings again and hits. Ains casts a spell and purple tendrils circle the area and float in the area, creating magical darkness - hitting the vines on Zymve and Ains and the bad tree next to him. Hap attacks the vine on him and misses, then attacks again and hits. He is able to swing again and fumbles. He then attacks one last time and hits. The tree in the purple circle takes damage and moves out of the circle. The other tree steps up and hits Aury twice doing big time damage, and roots burst up underneath him and he is grappled by them. The piercing damage from the roots inflicts more damage. Zymve breaks free of the root and moves away. She then gives temp hit points to herself, Ains, and Aury. She moves to the edge of the glade. Ky’tha shoots the tree holding Aury and hits with her crossbow and she moves to the edge of the glade. Aury casts a spell - radiance of the dawn - hitting all 4 vines, the tree on Aury, and the closest needle blight. The tree takes half damage. Needle blights take damage. Vines take damage (two go own). He also heals himself.
  • Round Two: Mist rises up and forms in the area in front of one of the trees, forming a stinking cloud (the area is heavily obscured). The vines take damage from the cloud. NB attacks Ains and hits; another attacks Og and hits; and another attacks Aury and misses. Ains is poisoned from the cloud and he casts shatter and kills two needle blights and a tree takes some damage and he tries to shove the tree, but it saves. Og has the poisoned condition. He attacks a vine and hits, killing it. He then runs outside of the cloud and hits the tree, dealing damage. Hap saves against the poison and runs out of the cloud to attack the tree and misses, then tries again and crits. Then misses again with his main weapon and hits again with the dagger. The tree on Og attacks and hits then attacks and misses, then roots emerge from the ground and he is grappled and will take piercing damage on his next turn. Zymve casts a spell on the tree on Og and it takes a lot of damage. She then inspires Og. The tree on Aury gnaws on him, inflicting damage. He takes damage from the cloud. He then tries to bash Hap and misses. He also misses Aury. A roots reaches out to grapple Hap, but he dodges the root. Kytha moves over to shoot the tree on Og and hits. Aury takes damage from the cloud and goes down.
  • Round Three: A pit opens up on Aury, but he is grappled and limp - the tree has him right now. A vine moves out of the cloud next to Ains, but can’t take an action. Og is grappled and tries to hit the tree and hits twice, dealing massive damage. Ains screams at the tree and it takes damage, and dies - and a body takes the place of the tree. Ains shoves the vine, but it doesn’t move. The seems to be the same infection that took over Diverra’s grove outside of Emberstran. Hap attacks the tree and hits and also inflicts menace on it - the tree is frightened and takes damage. He then hits with a dagger and attacks with crits with his his shadowtrail and is knocked prone and then hits again with a dagger. Zymve moves the daggers to the vine and it takes damage and dies. The tree pulls Aury out of the cloud and munches on him (death fail). He then stands up and attacks Hap w/disadvantage and misses and then fumbles and loses his reaction. He then tries to grab Hap and misses. Ky’tha moves back over and hits the tree with her crossbow. Aury then rolls a death save (we don’t know if it is a save or a fail).
  • Round Four: A new root pops up next to Og and Ains and Ky’tha and Hap. The needle blight teleports next to Og and claws at him with a crit, double damage. Hap, Ky’tha, and Og save against the vines; Ains is held. Ains attacks the needle blight with a sorcerer’s burst and hits with fire damage. Og attacks the needle blight and kills it. He then moves over to attack the tree and hits twice, felling it. Hap runs over to the priest and tries to heal him with his medicine kit. He is stabilized. Zymve dashes over, but can’t do anything. Ky’tha runs over to rifle through Aury’s pack and finds nothing to help. We decide that Aury needs to rest.

We check the tree - does it look better? It looks the same. The two creatures on the ground are shadowy looking. Hap thinks they are from some place not of this plane. We loot the bodies. They appear to be dark druids - looks like they are creatures from Blackrend. They have sickles, leather armor. They have studded leather that is black with black studs. Looks very nice. One wears a very nice cloak. Ains checks to see if they are magical. They are - the mantle is abjuration magic, the leather has abjuration magic. The building is also magical. Zymve takes the studded leather, while Ky’tha takes the mantle.

The altar in the building is magical. Og picks up Aury like a baby to carry him to the building. Hap approaches the building first with Ky’tha by his side. Hap sees through the open arch an altar inside that is covered with roses. Og steps forward, but feels his thornleaf pinching him. He hands Aury off to Hap. Hap, carrying Aury, walks in. Ky’tha follows. There are murals inside. Hap lays Aury on the altar. Radiant light burns down and Aury’s hands cross on his chest and his book appears on his chest with a new cover. Hap says “looka there - what that.”

Ky’tha looks around and investigates the area - checking out the carving. She thinks this is great artwork, well built. Aury heals for 1 point and he opens his eyes and feels warmth and more at home than he has ever felt. He thinks this is the OG temple to Diverra.

Ains follows Og to the tree. Zymve stands just outside the door of the Temple. Og hears a voice in his head - as if the thornleaf is speaking to him - it speaks without words but says “Answer its call,” as clear as day. He reaches for the tree with his hand. The bark is cool and rough. Og’s breathing slows and his eyes grow heavy. He hears “rest now Ogman Crag, accept your destiny and fall into E’espran.” He resists the pull. Ains sees him and asks, “what is going on.” He explains about resting - and they talk and Ains tells him to rest. Og puts his hand back on the tree. His skin starts to change. His hand looks like it is one with the tree…changing into bark. He looks like he is about to collapse. Ains helps set him down. He sees his eyes close heavily - he doesn’t think he is in danger - but he’s never seen him this calm. This might be bad - he doesn’t know. He tries to connect to Og’s thoughts. “Are you good?” Og says “ok” but is very tired. He feels connected to him, but he thinks Og is talking to something else. He hears:

The great cycle of green and shadow, the living pulse that binds the world's roots and branches–The Alferumn. It is more than a tree, a connection of all that grows and withers in balance. When the balance frays, the Alferumn suffers, and guardians are called. You are E’erspan, thorn-bearer, the one who walks between bloom and rot to restore what has been taken.

Og feels this thing’s pain. They share the pain. He has felt this before - this is the second time he has felt the blight. He feels that the tree’s roots are starved…it is poisoned. This seems to be the single most important tree - this is the Life Tree. It says “you feel the pain. Seek Dandelia of the Veil, for she has been seeking E’erspan’s aid.” He is being called forth to be the guardian of this tree, of life. He feels like he needs to find Dandelia (the druid of the Veil). This would be to the east of the Baltwood. (Kahlbit’s Veil). Ains thinks Og is going to be out for a long rest. Zymve sees this happening at the tree. She has never seen trees and she and Ains talk about trees. He explains that it looks like Og is okay, just resting.

Inside, Aury feels the weight of the tome on his chest - heavier than the book he brought with him. Radiant light shines on him. The words have been replaced with the words “Bloom Aeterna” - these are the original words of Diverra herself. This is the complete, accurate, unabridged version of Diverra’s teachings. Aury feels there must be answers in this book - he has the urge to rest and study the book. Ky’tha tells him what happened outside and that we don’t have the ability to heal him. Hap’s mind is blown as he realizes this was his original vision - why he was paired with Aury to begin with.

We decide to rest. Before resting, Zymve dons the armor (+1 studded leather); Ky’tha puts on the mantle (spell resistance). We take a long rest - Og and Ains by the tree; all else in the temple.

As we sit there, the skies darken - a new sensation for Zymve, but she’s in the temple.

Aury - he starts to read through the new tome. It is celestial. It reveals “I am Diverra the Ardent One.” It opens into an area that lays out stories that encompass beliefs in values. Passion, spark, beauty in its natural form. All beings hold divine potential to be nurtured. Inner beauty is the most beautiful. Then it goes into the tenants of Diverra. These are similar to what he has already learned - there are no differences, but it is more complete. Then the next piece is about divine teachings - how he projects this forward. Other pieces that stand include: Shattered Hearts - she talks about broken love, and how while she is Beauty, she is also the goddess of love.

##

"The Shattered Hearts, fragments of lost passion scattered across the world, must be sought and returned. When found, place each at the proper place of worship dedicated to me—shrines, temples, groves where my name is still honored. There, in sacred ground, their pieces may be reunited, their light restored, and the wounds of the heart healed. Only in this way shall the eternal bloom be made whole."

These are material components of love (which is immaterial). If someone comes across one they need to find out where it belongs. Aury would need to put it in its rightful place (praying to Diverra to find out where it goes). This is a sanctuary in its truest form. As long as he holds the book and specific petals he can cast a ritual and bring us back to this place. Anywhere in Myrdae…he can come back here. But he has to have petals and the book. This is the only copy of this book.

Ky’tha found on the altar a carving - hands offering a chalice of receiving. He needs to be looking for a chalice to place a shattered heart into. This specific place is the actual chalice he will need to use.

Zymve gets confused by Aury’s mumblings. She is not used to the surface. She is leaned up next to the archway. She hears everyone relax and rest, she hears whispers of a melody. No one else is responding to this. Maybe she is the only one hearing it and doesn’t know why it is just her. This melody is broken to everyone else, but to her it is complete and haunting and dark. It seems to be coming from outside. She looks outside and sees Og melded as one into the tree and a soft glow around Ains near his head and his eyes look pale in the starlight. And she sees him studying a book - he seems to be magically attached to the book. The melody seems to be coming from Ains. She sneaks over to him. She feels the music coming together. It reminds her of an early tune that she wrote.

Ains - in his mind the journal’s chapters are starting to move around and converge. Odd words are coming out. There is something about a catastrophe that came from hunger. Zymve sees that he is really intense. She thinks he looks like he is in pain. Words form as he is studying the book. What emerges are lyrics and she feels a song build inside her. She hears catastrophe and thinks this is like a title or chorus. She then hears “run, escape, lineage, bloodline” something along those lines (something innate is making you escape). She doesn’t realize that she’s on top of her. He closes the book, rubs his temples, and falls asleep. She picks up the book and the pages seem blank to her. A name pops into her mind: Szoradryn. This is the owner of the Silken Secrets. He is from Ys’nonterien. She thinks there is more to this book than Ains knows. She wonders why this drow has given her friend a book…and why it is having an impact on her. She is shaken up. What does this have to do with Ains, because it feels personal to me? She pockets the book, for now - hiding it in her backpack.

– The End of the Session –

WE END HERE. When we return, we will have had a long rest. Options - go back through the gate to where we just came from, take time to study the book to go through the portal to go back to the other Diverra grove. Need to decide Surface or back to Underdark?

The Chronicle of Emberstran · Chapter 20

Coming Home

In-World32nd of Talil, 1246 WhereBloomrest PlayedMarch 2, 2026

In Bloomrest (The Lost Sanctuary of Diverra)

We wake up. The tree that Og was attached to seems to have new life. It seems that the blight was driven back. Ky’tha tells the group that Lady Rook was elected…and is likely appreciative so a return to Emberstran would be in order. Need to check on Hap’s land; need for Aury to talk to his priestess. Hap and Aury pray and take a rose from the temple grounds using the incredibly powerful pruning dagger. It becomes a petrified rose that maintains its color. He takes 10 petals - pressed into the book.

Teleporting to Diverra’s Grove

We talk a little and then go to an opening in the Sanctuary. Aury starts the ritual using one of the petals, reading from the book. The petal floats around as we are all wrapped in radiant light…then it fades and we find ourselves back in Diverra’s Grove in the Baltwood. We follow the old hunting trail and look at the symbols carved into the trees to guide us back to Emberstran. Zymve notices this.

As we continue, we see three sweat-stained guys hefting logs onto a wagon. They recognize us and offer us a ride to the southern gate of Emberstran. These guys are Jorm, Veed, and Sigur (Emberstran Timber Company). They say they don’t recognize one of us and point to Zymve - Ains says she is dark and hides in the shadows and has always been with us. We climb aboard and start the walk back. Hap walks alongside with Fleur flying overhead. We ask about the last stretch - what news. They say the port and vineyards seem to be flourishing.

Brander & the Victors of the Vine

The cart takes us to an area with real, unfiltered sunlight as we travel to Emberstran. This is Zymve’s first view of a bright day. Ains creates and hands Zymve a pair of “sunglasses” to help shield them from the bright light. Every six seconds he recasts the spell to keep them up. These guys are making small talk with us. They say now that the blight is gone the lumber business is picking up. Eventually a young rider comes into view riding a horse and singing. His song has to do with the removal of the blight…

“No more the blight shall claim our blood, no more the fear shall reign,

For unseen hands have turned the tide—our thanks shall never wane!”

He then pulls next to us. He is Brander, a “wandering minstrel of no small repute”. That was the “Victors of the Vine” - tales of our deeds. He is shocked and pleased to have sung it to us. Zymve comments that it needs work. He says, well maybe so. He also comments how he thought there were 5 of us, but now sees that we are a group of 6. Ains tells the same tale…and in his head says “you are going along with this please.” Aury flips him a gold piece and Ky’tha tells him that more words rhyme with six than five. He then asks Zymve if she wants to be included in his next version and she tells him ‘to find another profession.’ And he leaves.

We are impressed by the speed and strength of the ox pulling the wagon. Ky’tha asks his name and he says he can’t since he will likely eat him someday. We start to come into view of the southern gate to the town.

Southern Gate of Emberstran

There are two guards there and they recognize us and are happy for our return. They wave us through. Ky’tha asks them to keep our return a secret - we’d like to surprise a few people…and pays them each 1gp to keep quiet. The cart pulls up to the Warm Bear Inn. We pay the guys 2gp to buy a round for the tavern this evening. And then give them another 2 gp to stay longer. Ains then messages her to tell her that he will be late. Zymve is overwhelmed, but stays quiet to observe everything.

We discuss who to visit. Hap and Ky’tha want to visit Lady Rook; Aury to the Temple, Ains, Zymve, and Og to Raddie’s Rarities. Then all meet back at the Dyed Cane. As we travel, we see that the town is busier and bustling. And people point to us and look at us. Zymve asks if there is a tailor - we comment that there is, and an armorer. She wants to buy a cloak with a hood to shield her identity and from the sun. She finds some and buys a black cloak. The others head to Raddic’s Rarities. This shop is full of odds and ends, manned by a half orc and a sharply dressed half elf. Ains asks him about a platinum vial that could hold a tentacle. He says he can have one for him tonight. Once they all make their purchases, they head to the Temple.

Ky’tha and Hap head to Imonorra’s Tower. We learn from the doorman that Lady Rook is at the Suncrest Cellarium. Normally the head would move to the Vintner’s Mansion, but she allowed Lady T (Tarathial Rulendor) to stay there. We ask how the transition of power went and he said it went as expected. He said he would send a message to Lady Rook to let us know we are coming. Im’s familiar appears and he gives him a message and he disappears. They decide to go to the Cellarium.

The Temple to Diverra

Meanwhile, the others head to the Temple. They discuss what Ains is trying to buy, but he doesn’t reveal what it is for. They proceed. They enter the Temple and see the Priestess Meladra. She is so happy to see them and grasps Aury’s hand and asks if he found the sanctuary/portal. He says he did and starts to tell her about all that transpired. He tells of trolls, an underground city…and the fact the portal takes you to the middle of the sea. She comments that it predates the Awakening. She sees a glow about Aury and comments as such. Ains comments that Aury is beating around the bush. Aury hesitates because he fears that it would be a difficult journey for others - particularly non-believers. He says it is in an isolated place that is likely to kill others. Zymve says it almost killed him. She says “you know you brought a drow back with you” and Zymve comments “you have a problem with that.” She backpetals and says no - we just don’t see many of you around here and apologizes, commenting that they typically don’t have the nicest of reputations. But if you are hanging with my beloved Aurilius then you are fine in my eyes. Aury sees Arnol edging towards Ains. The priestess asks what else did you find? Aury tells her about the blight we found and how Hap carried him to the Temple and was brought back by her. She asks what the experience was like. He says it is hard to describe, but tries. He then tells her about the Tree of Life and she was surprised to find out that it exists next to Diverra’s Sanctuary. He further describes the journey…start to finish. And tells that the blight might be spreading more in Myrdae. He then describes the Temple. He then talks to Arnol, asking if he has been doing the daily workouts with the captain. He responds that he has not been doing it daily and asks Aury not to question his convictions. He then bows and retires to his quarters…leaving quickly. The priestess responds that Arnol has not been himself, but that Diverra smiles upon Aury. Aury questions this…asking what is going on and she comments that he hasn’t been around much. He says he goes to training, but then leaves for hours at a time. Aury questions this, almost judging her. She says she does not want to be judged…that she has an entire city to take care of. Ains steps in to say that perhaps Arnol’s absence is more about puberty and learning. Zymve comments that the strong guidance of a woman makes a young man. Aury gets the sense that she is not covering for Arnol, but is really just excited to see Aury and for what he’s found. He thinks that she is just accepting of change and is concerned about Arnol, but is distracted and captivated by what Aury has found. He thinks she would be overwhelmed by the book he found - it would be a miracle - and decides not to share it at this time. He thinks that he has passed her in terms of favor with the goddess…and doesn’t want to overwhelm her. He inquires about his chambers and about additional rooms that might be available…and she says that space is always available for followers of Diverra. Og says - what about friends of followers and she says she could make an exception. Ains just asks her to use discretion, and she agrees.

The Suncrest Cellarium

Hap and Ky’tha head to the vineyards. Walk through the streets, recognized by guards. They are elusive about who and how many of the heroes are in town. They continue on and make it to the Suncrest Cellarium. Lady Rook is standing in a nook, set up like a tasting chamber. Wine-colored gown. She welcomes us and offers us a beverage. She steps through a door and returns with a scroll case - the deed to Hap’s property. She tells him that the Digglepot’s have offered to buy the property. He asks about his sister and she says that she never came. She comments that - looking at Ky’tha - some relationships have grown stronger and others weaker. She says your brother in law is a brother of the ruthless. He skipped town choosing your sister over his family. I have surrounded him with protection. She asked about the Marrowstone and said she’d like to have it for Emberstran. Ky’tha asks about the power of it and she comments that Imonorra has the conduit to bring it to its full potential. She says that the stone could be used to take down the Cloak of Defiance - she isn’t sure if it could take down Jerik, but thinks it could take most from the Crimson Swan. She says the Digglepots have offered 2,200gp for the land. She says that he shouldn’t bring back Phira from Trailpoint now, but with the stone, could make it safe to return her back. She says that affixing Imonorra’s rod to the stone will release the necrotic energy and allow Og to use the stone again. She offers us food and drink. Ky’tha asks about Lady T, the Lord, and other nobles and leaders. She says the Lord is ok - he knows where the power is - though the Cloak tried to shake him down. That Lady T wasn’t happy. Hap tells her that he is happy that Lady T was taken care of. Ky’tha agrees. She asks if Ky’tha has checked in with Tessa yet and says she should. Hap agrees to talk to Og about the stone. They leave and head to the Dyed Cane. Ky’tha notices a new sign that says Our Exquisites. This is a new shop - they head in to check it out. It is an arts studio. Wide space, pedestals, sculptures, abstract carvings. Smells new. Tessa bursts out and is happy to see Ky’tha. They briefly talk and agree to catch up later - that Ky’tha will stay there later.

The Dyed Cane

The rest of the group - heads to the Dyed Cane and talks about Arnol on the way. They arrive and Hap and Ky’tha arrive about 30 minutes later. They have already had a round of drinks. Ky’tha asks about how their morning fared - on the docks and at the Temple. Ky’tha comments on the cloak that Zymve bought - how she looks fetching in it. Ains talks about how the drow in the Underdark made better clothes. Bordi shows up and asks if we want the private room and we say yes. He asks about the tablet we found in his cask and we tell him that we are still piecing it together and will let him know once we have all the answers.

We get to the room. Hap tells Og and the group about Lady Rook’s proposition. Aury asks what Rook will do with the energy from the stone…and says he cannot condone those actions. Ky’tha comments that Phira will always be in danger - and probably Hap, too - as long as the Cloak thrives. Aury says that he doesn’t want any Diverra followers to be killed. Hap continues to lobby for the emptying of the stone…”how she empties the stone…ah…do we really care about that.” We discuss how it would actually be to Ains and Og’s advantage to take the Cloak down. Aury asks about Lady T’s fate and they share that she was allowed to stay. Og asks Zymve for her perspective. She agrees that being in favor of the one in power would be to our benefit. Hap says to consider this “law enforcement” and Aury agrees that he will turn a blind eye, though we may be “kicking the pine cone down the street.” The group agrees to give her the stone. The group advises Aury that he should go to the Temple tomorrow. We discuss that Ains will study the stone tonight and give Ky’tha the go ahead to contact Rook about meeting at the Tower.

Zymve goes with Ains and Og to the Warm Bear Inn to get info about Mount Emberstran. Hap and Aury plan to go downstairs at the Dyed Cane to eat and drink, while Ky’tha heads back to the new place to meet Tess. All but Ky’tha will stay at the Temple - she will stay with Tessa.

Our Exquisites

Ky’tha goes back to Our Exquisites to see Tessa and is escorted upstairs. There are others there - some are familiar, and she realizes that this is the new base for the Etterset and Tessa is the new face. She tells Ky’tha that she is welcome here and would like her to stay. Ky’tha declines, but says that she is happy to help. She responds that she’d like our help taking down the Cloak and Ky’tha tells her that they have plans with Lady Rook to do just that. Ky’tha asks her about the transition and then she asks about Zymve. Ky’tha tells her that she thinks she is running from something, but that she is to be trusted for now. Tessa says that she will put out feelers, but Ky’tha asks to do this with discretion since she may not want the attention, nor would the group want it.

Raddic’s Rarities

Ains, Zymve, and Og head to the drow….after Zymve disguises herself. Ains introduces her as “Og’s lady of the night.” Then they go to pick up the flask and here that it was purchased for 250gp. Ains tries to negotiate. He gets it down to 350gp, paying with 250gp and a 100gp ruby. He gets an eyeball. He then asks for a pickled tentacle. He doesn’t have one, but he gives him a jar, salt, and vinegar to pickle his own tentacle. They head to the docks to get one. He finally has all his ingredients - but doesn’t reveal for what. They then head to the Warm Bear Inn.

The Warm Bear Inn

Og apologizes about the lady of the night comment. At Warm Bear…the settle in. They notice the lumbermen…pacing themselves. A few people recognize them: “you are right, they are here.” Zymve is introduced as Og’s half-sister. They look around and Zymve and Ains notice a guy sitting in the corner with his head down, with a broken lute…several empty tankards–its Bander, the bard we met on the road to Emberstran. Zymve - disguised - offers to remedy the situation. Og gets chummy with some hunters, has a few drinks, and asks about Mount Emberstan - paths, threats, etc. A sturdy, strong woman sitting at the corner of the bar perks up and walks over and says “well I’ll be damned - heroes of Emberstran are going to Mount Emberstran” My name is Agnett and I’ve been traveling these parts for many years. She tells them it will be 3-5 days to get there. They will have to go through the Mist Springs. They are hot springs that create mists and make the trail hard to see…

“[The Mists are] beautiful, but they’ll hide your footing and sometimes show things that aren’t really there. Stick to the high ridges when the mist rolls in.”

Meanwhile, Zymve still disguised as a human mosies up to the bard Bander we met along the path and begins talking to him. She asks his deal, taking his drink away, he should be really experienced…(he had a bad day)... he says wrote a great song, but he didn’t get respect. She says he needs to lock in while she uses the Mending spell to mend his lute. He said he does well at every place he goes to, but those days are gone. She tries to convince him to play again. She pushes the lute into his chest…showing him some tips (giving him Bardic Inspiration) and he tries again and really plays well, impressing her. She offers to duet with him. He mentions that Zymve (who is disguise) is much nicer than the “bitch” he met on the road (referring to Zymve). They get up to play in the clearing and knock it out of the park (rolling a Natural 20 on Performance). Even Agnett pushes away to listen. The entire tavern is enthralled with their performance. They finish up and she asks his name. It is Brander, he announced himself as a wandering menstrual of no ill repute. She introduces herself as Lynda.

She wanders over to Ains and Og afterwards. Agnett then comes up and says “that was fabulous” to Zymve. Where were we? She talks about the creatures they will find. Sabers, black bears, boars, stags…in the lower areas. She says there are ash-covered monsters to be careful of…they look like swirling, skeletal figures. Ains asks about people. You haven’t heard the tale of Irragosa the Griever. She then tells them about it. The story was that some harmons ago, old Brynn was tracking a bore near the ash groves when an orange dragon dropped out of the skies. Scales were glistening with sea water. Snatched Brynn clean up and no one ever saw him again. Some say she is guarding something up there…others say she is looking for something. Always keep one eye to the sky. Og asked if anyone witnessed this. She says no. Ains tells Og in his head to stop asking questions. And tells Zymve in her head good job on the song. Agnett yells “Lynda, Lynda, Lynda…etc.” in admiration of the music. Og asks if there is anything else. She says to stay on the Old Trader’s Trail - it will take you to the base. But again, the springs will be a challenge, too. Ains gives her 3sp and says to have another round on us. Ains then says that they are all going up to their rooms upstairs. They say goodnight, as Agnett announces their departure. Ains casts invisibility on Og so that he can sneak back to the temple. Ains explains that he and Og are under attack from the Cloak which is why he is trying to fool everyone at the tavern.

Zymve replies that they need to talk. Zymve pulls out the Journal of Tsulanan that she snuck from Ains the night before and asks “where the hell did he get it and why does he have it?” He says that he got it from the guy at the Underdark town, but that it was one of his relatives' books. That his blood is special - that he is special - that he gets his powers innately. And that the book is answering questions about his power. He says he is surprised she has the book. Zymve threatens him that he needs to inform her about the information he gets from the book. He asks why she is interested in the book. She says she heard it and she didn’t like what it said. She says it is weird and came from a drow that came from my village. She asks what he knows of the drow. They talk about the evil goddess lady and spiders. She said that she thought the evil goddess lady won’t let her go. She ran away two years ago and no one has ever come for her, but now this book appears and she feels threatened by it. She says that she couldn’t have come to the surface on her own as she was fleeing. Ains asks her about their libraries where she could have gathered info. She says she just needed to get out of the Underdark….and the group was her ticket up. She feels like maybe she is ok. But wants to know if the book tells him anything that might be valuable to her. He agrees to tell her. He just says that his main concern here is about the Cloak and how they have bad favor with them, as well as other groups in the underbelly of the city. He says he will make her invisible so she can travel to the temple. She agrees. She says she has never had friends before and she doesn’t know what that’s about but if “you help me, I’ll help you.” He agrees and says that is what friends do…and how Og saved him before and how they became friends. He then tells Zymve about the Thornleaf that is attached to Og (and a funny story about the bathhouse and how Og spends a lot of time there staring at his leaf). She admits that she grew up in a cult where no one is a friend. They discuss cults. Ains turns her invisible and she heads to the Temple.

After she leaves, Ains studies the Marrowstone using the Scroll of Identify. He realizes that it is a two-part magic item - the stone and a rod. He knows that he should not touch the stone when it releases the energy. He thinks it would work like a Circle of Death; the radius and damage varies based on what is captured within. He knows he needs the rod to use it, but is trying to determine if there is a way to bypass the rod, using his own magical powers to release the energy. He thinks it could be possible, but might require more studies and be extremely difficult.

Zymve arrives at the temple…sees Og. Eventually, Hap and Aury arrive back at the Temple. Ains announces that he needs to use the restroom before going to bed at the Warm Bear Inn. He then makes himself invisible and heads to the Temple. He then appears 15 minutes later. A room has been prepared for everyone. Ains asks Zymve if she can inspire him…and she does. He then asks Aury if he can guide him in arcana. He then studies the Marrowstone with Bardic Inspiration from Zymve, Guidance from Aury, and his own Luck feat after returning to his room in the temple. Og and Zymve notice him studying it. They hear something like a crackle and look over and see a flash of darkness in the room…and when the darkness clears, Ains is holding the stone and it is empty.

– The End of the Session –

He Ate a Baby

In-World33rd of Talil, 1246 WhereEmberstran PlayedApril 8, 2026

The night before, when Ainslie absorbed the necrotic energy, everyone was in the Temple of Diverra. Ky’tha walked in right when Ainslie pulled the magical energy from the Marrowstone. Overwhelmed by the day's events and shocked by what occurred between Ainslie and the Marrowstone, the party decides to wait until the next day to discuss it.

34th of Talil, 1246

We all wake up in the Temple. Ky’tha confronts the group, especially Ains - Hap and I had promised the stone to Lady Rook to help take down the Cloak. Ains feels different today - like “something was scrubbed away from him” during the night (after the ritual with the marrowstone). The group thinks he looks different - he looks better and refreshed. He is trying to figure out what is different - why and how. He thinks this is something he cannot reverse. It may keep him youthful. Maybe that is why Lady Rook wanted it - to keep her youthful. Ky’tha wonders if consuming the marrowstone contents would help everyone or just Ainslee (or others like him). The group decides it may be something specific to Ainslee…and others like him (or who have a similar trait). The group discusses keeping the Marrowstone to ourselves. We know Blackrend is causing blight in the land…and we could use the marrowstone to resolve them as we encounter them.

There was a discussion about Og bonded with the Tree of Life. Is he able to sense where blight is in the world? He doesn’t appear to be able to yet; maybe his powers are still growing?

The group splits up:

  • Ains, Og, and Zymve are going to buy provisions - rations, rope, and pitons for the adventure to Emberstran Mount and to find the Emberheart Vault.
  • Aury is going to stay at the Temple and talk to the Priestess.
  • Hap and Ky’tha are going to see Lady Rook.

The plan is to meet up at the North Gate. As we are about to leave, the Priestess walks over to Aury and hands her a piece of folded leather. Aury then presents them with badges, it’s the Badges of Emberstran (the E.G.D.). Aury took them from their packs the night before and had them blessed by the Priestess.

Buying Provisions

Zymve goes to the leatherworking shop, introduces herself as Lynda and mentions her performance last night. The proprietor said she has been the talk of the town. She tries to sell the leather armor (he offers her 2gp) and tries to barter for a better price, intimidating the shopkeeper. He offers her 5 gp and a pair of leather mittens.

Og and Ains go to buy rations and rope. Ains walks in with spectacles that he created and rips them off. They are there on official business - heading to the mountains. They ask for supplies - the shopkeeper offers them at cost. Ains then asks for additional discounts, using persuasion. He is able to get a 50% discount (9gp instead of 17gp). They buy 5 bedrolls, 3 ropes. As they exit, a man walks by with a goat - the two decide to flash their badges and investigate the man and the goat. They don’t really find much. He says he was bringing down some potatoes and just delivered them. He says he is going back to his farm. They offer to buy a goat and bags for 10gp. He accepts. They ask if he has ever been to Mount Emberstran…and says no, and that he has heard about the orange dragon and the hunters who never came back.

Visiting Lady Rook at the Cellarium

Ky’tha and Hap go to see Lady Rook without the Marrowstone. We spot a shadow sitting on the rooftop nearby. Ky’tha catches a sign of red coloring and a red hooded cloak, with a glint of metal. As soon as she looks, he is gone in a flash. He was a Cloak of Defiance member, but no one we recognize specifically. Hap calls Fleur, his horned owl familiar, to circle above to help keep watch. She doesn’t see much. They pass Tessa as they are headed to the Cellarium. She greets Ky’tha, who reintroduces Hap. Another Etterset member shows up and more introductions. Ky’tha tells them about the Cloak member who was spying on us…and they are not surprised since word got around about us coming back to town. Tessa mentions that Hap came into something soon…and Ky’tha mentions that she hopes that no one else knows about that. Hap inquires about the people who want to buy his land. They say they are good people, potato farmers. Hap says he needs to talk to his sister first. They mention that Lady Rook threw a party last night - a great party. And that Rook is likely hung over. They knock on the door, no answer. Are just about to pick the lock, when the door opens and they see one of the captains of Emberstran. Ky and a Captain talk, he was at the party last night. He slept here, Ky wondered if he slept with Rook, but he seemed to be on the up and up. He reveals that Lady Rook was on the up and up. Hap tells the captain that Ainslie studied the stone and there was an accident and the stone is now empty, and we are going to refill the stone, and tells her to not be mad. The captain is confused, but low-key agrees to it without knowing better. Ky wants to try and join the next party if there is one when we return. They keep digging the hole deeper… Ky tells the captain that we are heading out of town, revealing we have a new companion… Hap says we will be back in a fortnite.

Ky’tha and Hap discuss further on the way out to the gate, how maybe that went better than expected. She can’t get mad about an accident, right? And who knows what the captain will actually convey to Lady Rook - he was very confused about the stone.

The Temple of Diverra

Aury talks to the priestess. He tells her the badges were great. He tries to decide if she will be stable enough to handle hearing of the book and thinks that she might be jealous - so he doesn’t show the book to her. He instead shows her a writing of the mission that Diverra has given to the group (about the hearts, he quotes in the poem). He asks her to write the poem down - the shattered heart mission. He takes out one of the petals from the sacred grove and gives it to her. He doesn’t explain the use of the petal - only that it came from there. She is excited to have one. [He does this to make sure that he always knows where at least one petal is]. He also talks to her about Arnol - he doesn’t think he is set for this profession. Aury said he thinks he is following a different path. She says she will study this further. Aury tells her he is not sure when the group will return. She asks if he has holy water for the trip - he does. She asks other questions about Arnol, about duties in the Temple. Aury provides guidance. She says that she thinks that Tarley is getting close to becoming a priestess and that she will look at bringing in another acolyte. Then he leaves.

Og and Ains go to the Old Oak & Grapes. As the head there, Og notices movement, but isn’t sure who it is (only that they are being sneaky). They go inside. Ains puts in a special request for carafes of wine for their trip. They buy three at 10gp each.

We all meet at the gate. Ky’tha asks about the goat (it is Reggie). It will help carry items.. Zymve shares her luck with the leatherworker. Ky’tha and Hap tell about their visit to Lady Rook. Some guards walk up and talk about when we are coming back. We decline to tell when we are coming back or where we are going.

Fleur circles above and then flies back and lands on Hap’s shoulder and continues. Ky’tha asks about if she poops. Fleur looks at us like we are crazy…of course she doesn’t.

Magic Goat Bags

We step out of the gates. When we do, we no longer have a goat, but instead we have a gertot (an antlered sheep that is livestock in Mydrae raised for its wool and food). Ains detects magic on the saddlebags. Ky’tha looks through the bag. We don’t find anything on the bags that is out of the ordinary. Aury checks the gertot out. We deduce that maybe it had magic on it that protected it from being a gertot in town…since they are often on the menu. We ask if it had a spell on it to protect it and it nods yes. We ask about his name and find that it is Ewing. He motions to Og to walk through the gates with the bags on him…and he does and turns into a goat. He comes back out of the gates and turns back into himself. Og puts the bags back on Ewing. Aury asks if he minds carrying the bags - he doesn’t.

The Red Rider

A rider shows up on a horse…he is wearing a red cloak. He threatens the group - says that we shouldn’t have come back. That Jerik now has our scent and will not forget. Ains shoots a lightning bolt, Og throws a hand ax and knocks him off the horse, and Ky’tha shoots a bolt that misses. The lightning killed him. The horse runs back to town. A lady runs into a cottage and slams her door. She is scared of the group since she saw the killing. Og and Ains pull out their Emberstran badges and say he was a wanted criminal and they needed to take care of him. They are able to deceive her and her husband. They ask what the guy did and Ains replies that “he ate a baby” and they are happy that he was killed. Ains offers them 10sp for personal distress. The townspeople are both horrified and elated at the news. They convince them to keep this confidential - for their safety. Ferdinand (a neighbor) offers to dispose of the body for us (to bury them with others he has). Instead we throw the body to the pig. We take the sword and dagger off the body. Ains messages the guard to take the riderless horse into custody - official business. We move along past the last cottages of Emberstran.

Into the Baltwood

We move into the Baltwood. As we go, Ains feels like he is really in his prime. We travel for a couple of hours. We see deer grazing. And keep moving. We find a clearing and set up camp. We see Mount Emberstran and think we are heading in the right direction. Hap asks Ains to send a message to Phira: “Hap says looking forward to meeting you. Jerik is angry. Take precautions. Also, inherited a vineyard - interested in running it?” She replies: “It would be great to see you again. Are you headed this way? I don’t plan to run a vineyard - I have an Inn in Trailpoint.” The camp watch goes in this order:

1. Fleur on alert in the trees all night.

2. First watch – Aury reads the book from Diverra, and Ky’tha looks around.

3. Second watch – Ains sees what is around the fire; Zymve can see 120’. Ains talks to her about biscuits…and how he didn’t mean to offend her earlier talking about mushrooms. He asks if she is homesick? And takes out a carafe of wine and gives it to her. He says he would not recommend touching his book again. He asks her for inspiration as he casts Sending to Arnol (trying to be an evil wizard): “He mentions looking into an artifact known as the marrowstone and keeping the information to himself.” Arnol replied confidentially (Ains said he will be safe but gone for some time).

4. Third watch – Hap and Og talk, and bring up Hap’s sword. Hap talks to his sword, it warms up in his hands and it replies, “acknowledged”. He’s a bit put off by the talking sword and doesn’t talk to it much. Hap can't bring himself to talk to the sword further.

35th of Talil, 1246

It's the next morning now, the crisp air lets us know the day will be cool again. An invisible hand wakes Ewing and Ainslie asks if he can graze. He also casts extended Mage Armor on it during the rest. We all got a long rest. The trails get harder to follow and Og and Aury take point with Zymve inspiring ahead of the survival check. We go all the way to a stream, find claw marks made by a bipedal creature, its not a gnoll, nor a bear, looks like it might have come down on all 4 feet. We think this might be a creature as big or bigger than Og, based on the stance. We all study the tracks and the only thing that comes to mind is they are similar to the creatures in Nungulthil at the bar (the quaggoths). Ky’tha and Hap recall tales from the Underdark where beasts came up hundreds of years ago - maybe this is a creature like that.

We continue to move on the trail, traveling for a few hours. It is close to twilight. We knew it was going to take a while to get to the Mountain. We see a trail open up ahead as we think we are approaching the foothills. We see a motionless humanoid at the base of a cliff about 90’ ahead. Ky’tha stealths ahead to check out the body; Og following behind. She gets about 30’ from the body and sees he is wearing leather, but has a smashed head, likely from falling. She also sees something following Og. She pauses a moment to watch and no longer sees it. She yells out “something is following you.”

IT IS INITIATIVE
  • Round One: A creature jumps out to attack Og. This is a quarath, the surface version of a quaggoth - that came up a long time ago and has evolved. It hits him with a claw and then misses. Another one jumps out at Aury from the shadows and misses with first claw and then Aury releases a warding flare and he still hits. Og rages and attacks, hitting it well. Ky’tha jumps in to attack the one on Og and kills it with her dagger. Hap attacks the one on Aury, with a flyby from Fleur, and misses. The tries to hit with the pruning dagger and does and then he frightens him with a short sword, hitting him. Then bonus action uses the dagger and hits again. Aury flashes his holy symbol and casts radiance of the dawn and will take full damage. Ains hits him with a sorcerer’s burst and kills him. We hear leaves shudder in the distance and we see 4 similar creatures take off and scatter. Out of combat.

These creatures don’t have any gear - they are just natural creatures. Ky’tha takes a trophy of a claw from the one who attacked Og. Hap reveals from his memory that these creatures supposedly created a town…so it is possible that the four who fled will come back with more.

The figure at the bottom of the rocks is a half-elven male. We think he was probably thrown from the top of the cliff by one of these creatures. He has a silvered short sword, we see a finely crafted hand crossbow with bolts (which has an odd, oily sheen). No markings to identify him. Ains detects that the oily sheen bolt is magical, hand crossbow is magical. Pouch has 12gp, 14sp, 23 cp. Sack has food (3 days of rations) and a piece of paper looks to be recently written: “orange shadow sited twice over the grove of ash, too clever for a beast, keeps to the heights near the old sealed way, warn the others.” We create a cairn as Aury blesses her.

The path leads over the cliff - we follow it up. The cliff is made out of lava stone (black and dark). At the top, Aury tells us we should continue NE (the creature came from the north). There is evidence of a struggle here of more than one of these creatures. We still think we are another day or two away. We continue on the trail and press on. We see a campsite that looks recently used by the half-elf. We don’t think we have been followed. We set up camp with the same watch order:

  • First watch – Ky’tha notices the blue moon in the sky; Aury does more reading to compare the differences in what he learned and the original text. He learns that her followers come from all over. She may be a goddess of many (many petals on a rose).
  • Second watch – Ains is reading and Zymve hears the chant (of her dreams). This is inspirational to her - it does not make her uneasy. She feels inspired to create new songs, new words, particularly to create comfort. This starts to put her angst at ease. She is looking forward to hearing the songs Ains creates. She realizes that no one else can hear what she hears. She peeks at the book as Ains is reading and the pages are still blank to her. She thinks that maybe a drow was a companion of the one who wrote the book. Thinks there are similarities between the words in the story and her lived experience with Ains. Bottom line - his studies bring her comfort. As Ains gets to the third part, the words don’t make sense, but the meaning of “flight” comes to mind - related to honor (escape was in the second section). He can tell that something shifted dramatically in the life of the writer. As he is about to doze off, he goes back to the second part and he takes another look and he can't put his finger on this, but he thinks there is something to do with a calling. Someone felt like they owed something to him - they sought him out. After his time of reading and reflection, he talks to Zymve. She says she heard something. He tells her what he read. It is confusing. He then asks what her deal is. She asks about his deal. He reveals that the dragon is supposed to give him information. She says that she is wanted by groups. He offers to send messages to any friends or family she has - she says she has no one to contact. Ains sends a message to Phira before going back to sleep: “No need to come to Emberstran. Enjoy your Inn. Hap was just offering. Sorry to wake you. Jerik’s a bad man. He is after Hap. Night.” He gets a message back from Phira (he keeps it to himself). Then he casts another spell: “No need. All good. He is 100% safe. If he dies you can blame Aurelius, a priest of Diverra. Stay safe.” She replies to him. He then sends another message: “Don’t. I’m sorry I sent anything. Have a good night. Don’t tell Lady Rook. You don’t need to respond.”
  • Third watch – Then Hap and Og wake up. Ains tells Hap that Phira is dangerous. Asks if his brother-in-law is strong. He tells him that Phira said that “Aury’s name will be carved in blood on the very blade that takes him” should anything happen to Hap. Hap now thinks there is more to the story with Phira. She may be as much or more of a badass than her husband. They then go to sleep.

Og offers some suggestions of how to talk to his sword. Hap asks the sword if it has a name. He hears “you are not prepared.” [The name] will come to you. He asks “are you mad that we killed the dragon?” No - but I sense it. Hap says “The dragon?” It says: “Goodnight, Hap.”

The fire starts to burn low and Og is looking up and he sees the moon. Then suddenly, a silhouette goes across the moon - it is a ship flying in the sky. He points this out to Hap. They can both see it flying in the sky. Deep wing beats start to make noise above the adjacent tree tops. A vast shadow sweeps over the campsite. The wind sparks up the campfire and leaves blow off the trees. They catch a brief glimpse of orange scales as they hear it make a screech that echoes through the forest as it heads for the ship. Og, Hap, Fleur and the sword are the only ones who saw the dragon.

WE END HERE. It is dawn, 36th of Talil, 1246

The Chronicle of Emberstran · Chapter 22
22

The Tracker

In-World36th of Talil, 1246 WhereOld Trader’s Trail en route to Mount Emberstran PlayedApril 13, 2026

We all comment about “what was that sound” and “what is that flying ship?” As we look, we see the dragon hit the ship with lightning as it closes on the ship. Hap heard rumors of a ship that “years ago people crashed in Myrdae in a flying ship” and also that there was a flying cloud castle over Basctdelm - but think these are rumors and tall tales. We look at the dragon slam into the ship and wrap around it - trying to collapse it. We see a person fall from the ship as it tilts. We see the dragon rip off the front of the ship. We see both the ship and dragon tumble down ending in a loud crash in the distance. This crash was to the west - not in the direction we were heading.

We discuss going to the crash site or continuing on to find the Emberheart Vault. Hap sends Fleur up and can’t see anything. Aury and Og think it would take several hours off the trail to get to the crash site. Ains and Ky’tha think of dragons that shoot lightning - bronze and blue dragons and that bronze dragons frequent water (the dragon they saw was dripping wet). We think we didn’t get a good view of the dragon, but think this is a bronze dragon - and likely the “orange” dragon that we are seeking.

Ains decides to try to use a Sending spell to contact the dragon. Ky’tha climbs up a tall tree to keep watch - looking for signs of the dragon taking flight, smoke, or anything in the distance. She sees the dragon take flight and yells down to everyone and then it disappears in the direction of Mount Emberstran as she looks back up. Meanwhile, Ains gets a message back and the dragon doesn’t think Ains is Ains - but instead her master. The group asks if he sounds like his parents - he does. We decide to head in the direction of Mount Emberstran.

Being Followed

Og leads the group as we follow the trail. After 20-30 minutes, Fleur messages Hap and says “hoo, hoo (I think I caught a glimpse of the dragon flying low).” Og leads, followed by Aury, Ky’tha, Zymve, Hap, Ains and Ewing. Every now and then Hap closes his eyes to see through Fleur’s eyes - and then when he opens his eyes he catches the sound of measured breathing next to him. He feels like it is off the trail - this gives him an odd sense. He doesn’t say anything to the group, but decides to track it himself. After a few minutes, the sound is gone. Ky’tha decides to go over to see if there are any footprints. She finds evidence that someone was on the trail - someone was following and trying to be sneaky. The group continues forward and hears the sound of water - they approach a wide river after the round a bend. Ky’tha continues to track the evidence of someone following the group. She looks around and doesn’t see any animals - nothing around.

River Crossing

The group sees steam come off the water, feels heat coming from the water. As they approach the water, Og sees a large log fallen across the river and almost makes it to the other bank. Looking downstream, they see small rapids downstream. As he gets closer, he sees scales darting around under the water - orange and bronze colored. He comments that he isn’t sure what he sees - a dragon, fish? This river is big enough to support the dragon. The scales are moving. He comments on what he sees and Aury steps forward to look at the water. Aury sees fish in the water with the orange/bronze scales. He realizes that the color changes because of the water. Aury reaches down to touch the water and takes fire damage - the water is boiling - as he yells out. Og tries to spear a fish from the bank with his glaive. He hits it but it isn’t a clean pull - he is able to throw it onto the land. It is a “fire-colored” scaled fish with a sucker mouth and fanged teeth.

Ky’tha continues to the water and loses the trail - no evidence here of anyone else. She comes out of stealth and lets the group know of what she found. They tell her about the fish.

Discuss the need to figure out how to cross. We decide to cross with 2 ropes tied together and tie off…and Aury takes Ewing and flies across, sprouting wings and praying as he crosses over the boiling river. He gets to the other side and ties the rope off. Zymve decides to go first and grabs the rope and runs across the log and makes it. While she crosses, Aury flies up to see above the canopy. The Og gets large and takes Ains in his arms and tries to cross with both of them. He slips but catches his footing…and falls to the other side safely. Ains thinks that he heard Og laugh…and confronts him about it. Aury sees a shadow disappearing behind the mountain - and notices a dragon in the distance. Hap decides to cross at the rocks and stumbles, but catches himself…then finishes gracefully. Ky’tha unties the rope and crosses the log without advantage…rolling up the rope as she goes - very gracefully.

The Tracker

We continue forward on the trail - same order as before. As twilight starts to fall, we look for a campsite. Og finds a clearing. We think we could be as close as a day or two from the base of the mountain. Ky’tha hears heavy breathing from ahead of her - and realizes it is not Aury. She points this out to the group. Zymve starts to cast a spell and fairy fire erupts in front of Ky’tha…red lights up and catches a foot of someone fleeing. Ky’tha yells for it to stop and it doesn’t. Aury casts a spell and can see invisible creatures…and can see where the hooded figure is. He whispers to Ky’tha where the figure is. She diverts to go “in another direction” and then goes into stealth - trying to approach the figure based on where Aury said. As Ky’tha approaches the area, the figure moves. Aury says out loud - I can see you behind the fourth tree on the left. He sees the figure step out from behind the tree, pull the hood down. This is a blond elf - she eyes Aury and then turns and walks off in the other direction. Aury gives her a thumbs up and shows he can see her. Ky’tha tries to track her and catches evidence here and there of her movement. Finally at a spot 30’ away she sees a figure sitting next to a tree, reaching into a satchel - doesn’t think she spots her. It pulls out a flask and looks back at the direction of the camp. She sees Ky’tha and disappears. Ky’tha tries to convince her to stop and stay. She throws two daggers at Ky’tha’s feet then comes to retrieve them (invisibly) and Ky’tha grabs her. They begin talking back and forth, and Ky tries to get her to reveal information… Ky tries to find out who “She” is when Petal (the blonde wood elf that we are talking to) mentions the Etterset and that “she wants us to succeed”. Ky gets the impression that she works for Lady Rook. Ky’tha tells her “tell the Lady that I said hello.” Petal smiles and leaves. As she goes away, Ky asks for and gets an invisibility potion from Petal. Ky returns to the group.

Ky’tha tells them what she learned - that she thinks that Petal worked for Lady Rook. The group discusses how and why the Lady Rook would want us to succeed. Ains talks about how he had discussions with Rook about the vault - and about his conversation with Master An’davo. He also then tells the group that he has the ability to be invisible if needed. The group talks about Lady Rook. Zymve and Aury have major reservations about Lady Rook. Ains and Zymve make small talk around the camp. Ains shares that one of his ancestors rode Irragosa. He shares that there were dragonriders of Myrdae - that this wasn’t a tall tale. Aury knows from history that occasionally the team of dragon and rider were formidable in war. There was a dragonrider that lived on an island on an oversized lake that after the Awakening became the Glimmering Sea. Riders were few and far between - there are not many in this world. Dragonriders were rare, and many considered them more legendary than a King. No one has really spoken on dragonriders since the Awakening. The group confronts Ains about his link to a dragonrider. He tries to explain that he shares the same blood as the dragonrider. Aury thinks the story doesn’t add up. He thinks Ains believes what he says, but doesn’t think that would link them. Ky’tha asks if it is possible that Ains was lied to his entire life. He doesn’t think so. Ains decides to cast Sending to Ehkel an’Davo. He responds that Lady Rook wants what is in the vault and he doesn’t know what it is. Also that the dragon is likely guarding the vault. Ains knows that An’davo is in lock step with Rook, but that maybe a better offer might break his loyalty. So they keep watch and get rest.

  • First watch – Hap and Aury. Aury sees a shadow far off in the distance, sitting next to a tree, watching the group. He knows it is Petal observing us. He gives her a look to let her know that he sees her. She pulls off her hood and gives him a “what do you want to do” look. He notes that it is curious that a wood elf has blonde hair. He shares this with Hap. When he turns to look back at her, he sees that she is walking toward camp. She stops about 10’ from camp. “I have to watch you. I know you can see me. But I have to watch you. It is just a watch. She knows where you are going, she just wants to make sure you get there. I’m being paid 650gp to watch you.” Aury talks to her and Hap walks up. Aury tells her she is getting paid well and that he reminds her that the group acts on their own. He tells her that he prefers that she watches with them knowing and she says okay and walks off. Hap hears “Yo” from his sword. He says hello. It says “I feel it…we are closer” Hap says “to” and it responds “neverending.” He shakes his head and scratches his head, confused. He is also impatient. “We should have it.” Hap says “Look buddy, I don’t like riddles. You are using pronouns without antecedents.” It says “You’ll see…trust me.” And he says “I’m sure you will tell me what you are going to tell me when you tell me.” And then goes back to the watch. They wake up Ains and Zymve.
  • Second watch – Ains sends a message. He also reads his book. - he fake reads it - to see if Zymve is watching him. He’s deep in thought and discombobulated. He seems very confused. Then he reads the book for real for an hour. She hears the beats she likes. She knows that when she feels that vibe she will sleep better. She realizes that giving the journal back to him was a good thing - it opens her mind and she starts to notice and connect with more around her and a zen moment of who she is. Ains starts to get a vision of a strange, large construction effort - digging, building, stones being removed. There was a calling of dwarves to the east to come forth and dig. They are building things. As Ains tunes into the pages, they are about construction of something deep underground. As he awakens, he is going into a mountain. They then have their watch. Ains shares that his ancestor is Tsulanan…he is the dragon rider. He is the one who wrote the journal. She asks him how the drow ended up with the journal. All he knows is that his ancestor and the drow were best friends. She encourages him to read more from the book. He also shares that the contents of the vault belong to Ains (by inheritance). They agree that Lady Rook and her spies should not have access to the contents of the vault. He says that he will “kill anyone who stands in his way” of getting the vault contents. He is trying to freak out the spy. He tells Zymve in her head that he is trying to scare the spy. Neither see the spy. He responds in her head that he wishes he knew more about the spy. She responds that she agrees. He is casting detect thoughts in the area to see if he can pick up on the spy. Spell allows thoughts in a 30’ area to be detected. He picks up that the spy is close by. He asks Zymve to inspire him…and she pulls out an instrument to play. He then says out loud “You know not who you are dealing with. Reveal yourself to me or I will exterminate you from this land.” He tries to intimidate the spy. He sees leaves crumble in front of him and a hood pulled back. She reveals herself. “I don’t want to be in the middle of this - I’m just doing a job.” He detects thoughts - “what is in this place. What does she (Rook) want?” She’s looking around. “I’m just trying to do my job” Zymve tells her that she should find a new job. Ains probes deeper into her thoughts. He sees a play-by-play session: she and Rook at the Tower, eating, talking, these people are important to me (says Rook), they are going to go find what I need. I’ve shown restraint and they are going to get what I need. But I need you to follow them to make sure they are successful. I need you to interrupt/intervene to help them if you can. If you can’t, then bring me the marrowstone and the tablet. She is given a purse and a sack of gold from Rook. Do you best not to let them catch you - they are talented. If they find what I need, return it to me at once.” In her head, Zymve hears from Ains - please intimidate her to leave now and never return to Emberstran. Ains makes sparks appear in his hand to assist her. Zymve then grabs the front of her cloak - “you are going to leave, you are not going back to Emberstran, you are going to appreciate the gold you have already been given, and go on with your life - do you understand?” She steps back as Zymve holds the cloak. Her eyes are wide. “If I see you again, I will kill you,” She drops the purse on the floor, nods, turns and leaves. Ains is trying to determine what she is thinking “It wasn’t supposed to be like this.” She starts to leave. Once out of site, Ains tells Zymve that he hopes that was the right call. They each get a potion of invisibility from the dropped purse. Zymve gives him grief that he didn’t kill her (implying that we are a group of do-gooders). He ends up running after her and giving her one of the potions - in a nice gesture to make sure she made it out of there safely. She takes it, shows him she still had one, and leaves. Ains feels okay with his actions - he really didn’t mean her harm. He casts a final detect thoughts - he thinks she is not going to Emberstran, nor to talk to Lady Rook. Instead to Kalhbit’s Vale. He then shouts “one more thing.” Do you know a Dandelia? I’m not so sure (but her thoughts show that she does). He says “we will be seeing you again, I assume.” She says, “I hope not” and turns and leaves.
  • Third watch – They then wake up Ky’tha and Og. They share that they “ran off the spy” and will not likely see her again. Ains then sends a final sending spell to the spy “ha ha ha” like she laughed at him when he almost fell in the water. Og and Ky’tha talk about Rook - maybe the spy was a decoy and Rook has more up her sleeve. No other action that night.

37th of Talil, 1246

The next day we are walking. Aury swears that he heard a twig snap. Then thinks he heard a leaf drop. We continue moving along. He thinks he felt something touch him, but isn’t sure. He thinks that someone is there. He casts see invisibility and looks around. He spots the spy - she is close. We come to a fork in the trail:

  • To the left the path ascends, going up to the lava/rock/ashen area up the Mountain (where the dragon flew).
  • To the right, it is level, slightly descending, and more of a well-worn path.

Aury pulls out the tablet and looks around. He feels a warmth and vibration to the right. He tells the group we need to go right. And then points out “our friend” and Zymve yells “you bitch.” He sees her 45’ behind the group, behind a tree. Aury casts fairie fire on top of her…and she saves. She then takes off, Og takes off, and Aury casts it again (saving again). He is pointing her out to the group as she runs. Og is running where Aury is pointing. Og sees the evidence of twigs and branches since she is dashing. He swings to attack and rolls a crit. She is knocked prone and he hits her hard. She sits up bleeding, she begs for mercy. We all see her now. He puts weight on her to keep her down. Ains mentions to her about “manners” which was part of their sending. Zymve pulls out a dagger: "What did I tell you, bitch. I told you to run with the gold you already have.” Ains looks through her bag - there are no additional potions. He says “we don’t have to kill her now” - Zymve mentions that she needs no mercy, no additional chances. She plays on the emotions of Zymve - “why are you here - is it not strange - this is the way they treated me in the Bathean Empire.” Ky’tha jumps in - has Lady Rook communicated with you at all or anyone else? She says no. Ky’tha tells her that we have not promised Rook anything and that no one gets what is in the vault until we see it. She says - I just need to make sure you get to the vault. I will just share your success. I will just escort you and then leave. Ky’tha intervenes for the spy - “what’s the harm of her escorting us?” Aury and Zymve both want her dead and/or gone. She looks at Zymve “you don’t know what it is like to make promises (as a drow)” She commands the spy to “sit down” and she does. Og grabs her.

IT IS INITIATIVE
  • Round One: Ky’tha tells her to drop her dagger (intimidation) and she does. Ky’tha picks it up. Ky’tha tells the group that this needs to end now. That no one needs to die and she can come with us - that she was only doing her job. Aury says that he is not going any further if she goes, too. She then slips away and tries to run. Og’s reaction is to try to punch her and she drops prone again. She falls and is dazed. Hap asks “what do y’all want to do with her” and ties her up with rope.

The group has a discussion. Aury feels like she would go to the vault and then betray us. Zymve wants to kill her (wants to pull a Hewson). She pulls out a dagger and walks up to slit her throat. Og is holding her and Zymve kills her and says “I did this for all of you, since none of you could do it. You can sit around and bicker all day, but she wasn’t going to stop.” Hap says “she makes a good point.” Ains checks her pouch and there is 650gp. A wood elf lies dead in front of us. Ky’tha goes into stealth (29) and starts walking down the trail to the right. Ains takes his spell to make it look like she was killed from a lightning bolt vs. a stab - he does this with great surgical skill - making it look like the dragon burned her. Then he grabs the last potion of invisibility and turns to the left. Hap asks for a shovel and wants to bury the dead girl. Og says a prayer and follows Ains. They think Ky’tha is mad…is she? Or maybe she just wanted to duck out from this situation and not be part of the burial or looting of her. Ains tries to feel an innate pull one direction or the other - his blood tells him to go both ways. Zymve states that talking to the dragon might be best. Ains thinks that the dragon might not be reasonable - we better be ready for the conversation when we get there.

The group sits at the fork and decides to take the path to the right…

– The End of the Session –
The Chronicle of Emberstran · Chapter 23
